📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Nature medicine  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-26  |  DOI: 10.1038/s41591-026-04754-3  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026

集成废水和临床 基因组监测,用于 麻疹病毒 进化 和 传播 的高分辨率跟踪。

Integrated wastewater and clinical genomic surveillance for high-resolution tracking of measles virus evolution and transmission.
作者： Sipho Gwala, Joshua I. Levy, Victor Vusi Mabasa, Kathleen Subramoney, Nkosenhle L. Ndlovu, Chris Kent, Maryam Ahmadi Jeshvaghane, Praneeth Gangavarapu, Menzi Sikakane, Natasha Singh
摘要中文翻译摘要 麻疹 暴发 在全球范围内激增,对 监测 和控制力度提出了挑战。在南非,2024−2025 年 麻疹 暴发 导致报告了近 4,000 起病例,引发了全国范围内的 疫苗接种 运动。然而,大规模追踪 麻疹病毒(MeV;人麻疹病毒属)传播 和 进化 仍然很困难,特别是在护理、诊断测试和测序能力有限的环境中。废水可以产生群体规模的病毒 监测,但低 MeV 浓度限制了检测灵敏度和测序质量。在这里,我们表明,克服这些技术挑战可以实现废水和临床综合 基因组监测,从而扩大 MeV 监测的覆盖范围和分辨率。我们开发了病毒浓缩和全 基因组 测序 (WGS) 工作流程,并将其应用于 2024 年至 2025 年 基因组 期间在南非所有九个省收集的 4,502 个废水样本。废水 MeV 基因型 患病率 跟踪了临床估计,结合废水和临床序列的系统发育分析揭示了 传播 的地理结构和以前未检测到的省际传播。WGS 恢复了现场标准方法未捕获的 传播 链接,该方法针对核蛋白基因 (N450) 的 450 个核苷酸区域,突出了 WGS 对于准确表征 传播 动力学的重要性。我们的研究提供了一种经济高效且可扩展的 基因组监测 方法来支持 麻疹 的控制和消除。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 暴发 在全球范围内飙升,对 监测 和控制努力提出了挑战。该研究旨在解决大规模追踪 麻疹病毒(MeV;人麻疹病毒种)传播 和 进化 的困难。
设计与对象
该研究于 2024−2025 年 麻疹 暴发 期间在南非进行,报告了近 4,000 例病例,并引发了全国范围内的 疫苗接种 运动。
核心方法
研究人员开发了病毒浓缩和全 基因组 测序 (WGS) 工作流程,并将其应用于 2024 年至 2025 年 基因组 期间在南非所有九个省收集的 4,502 个废水样本。
主要结果
废水 MeV 基因型 患病率 跟踪了临床估计,结合废水和临床序列的系统发育分析揭示了 传播 的地理结构和以前未检测到的省际传播。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,集成废水和临床 基因组监测 可以扩大 MeV 监测的覆盖范围和分辨率,从而更准确地表征 传播 动力学。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了一种经济高效且可扩展的 基因组监测 方法,可以支持 麻疹 的控制和消除工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据受到检测废水中低浓度 MeV 的技术挑战的限制,这可能会影响结果的灵敏度和测序质量。证据强度中等。

Integrated wastewater and clinical genomic surveillance for high-resolution tracking of measles virus evolution and transmission.

Authors: Sipho Gwala, Joshua I. Levy, Victor Vusi Mabasa, Kathleen Subramoney, Nkosenhle L. Ndlovu, Chris Kent, Maryam Ahmadi Jeshvaghane, Praneeth Gangavarapu, Menzi Sikakane, Natasha Singh
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Measles outbreaks have surged globally, challenging surveillance and control efforts. In South Africa, the 2024−2025 measles outbreak led to nearly 4,000 reported cases, triggering a nationwide vaccination campaign. However, tracking measles virus (MeV; species Morbillivirus hominis ) transmission and evolution remains difficult at scale, particularly in settings with limited access to care, diagnostic testing and sequencing capacity. Wastewater can enable population-scale virus surveillance, but low MeV concentrations limit detection sensitivity and sequencing quality. Here we show that overcoming these technical challenges enables integrated wastewater and clinical genomic surveillance that expands the coverage and resolution of MeV monitoring. We developed a virus concentration and whole-genome sequencing (WGS) workflow and applied it to 4,502 wastewater samples collected across all nine provinces of South Africa during the 2024−2025 outbreak. Wastewater MeV genotype prevalence tracked clinical estimates, and phylogenetic analyses combining wastewater and clinical sequences revealed geographically structured transmission and previously undetected inter-provincial spread. WGS recovered transmission links not captured by the field standard approach, which targets a 450-nucleotide region of the nucleoprotein gene (N450), highlighting the importance of WGS to accurately characterize transmission dynamics. Our study provides a cost-effective and scalable genomic surveillance approach to support measles control and elimination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles outbreaks have surged globally, challenging surveillance and control efforts. The study aimed to address the difficulty of tracking measles virus (MeV; species Morbillivirus hominis) transmission and evolution at scale.
Design and population
The study was conducted in South Africa during the 2024−2025 measles outbreak, which led to nearly 4,000 reported cases and triggered a nationwide vaccination campaign.
Core methods
The researchers developed a virus concentration and whole-genome sequencing (WGS) workflow and applied it to 4,502 wastewater samples collected across all nine provinces of South Africa during the 2024−2025 outbreak.
Main results
Wastewater MeV genotype prevalence tracked clinical estimates, and phylogenetic analyses combining wastewater and clinical sequences revealed geographically structured transmission and previously undetected inter-provincial spread.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that integrated wastewater and clinical genomic surveillance can expand the coverage and resolution of MeV monitoring, offering a more accurate characterization of transmission dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides a cost-effective and scalable genomic surveillance approach that can support measles control and elimination efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by the technical challenges of detecting low concentrations of MeV in wastewater, which may affect the sensitivity and sequencing quality of the results. The evidence strength is moderate.

Journal: Public health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-23  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.puhe.2026.106545  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 261, 106545

北美麻疹逆转:加拿大失去出线资格,美国紧随其后。

North America's measles reversal: Canada has lost elimination status, and the United States is next.
作者： Sten H. Vermund, Rumour P. Piepenbrink, Elizabeth M. Traverse
摘要中文翻译美洲地区于 2016 年实现了 麻疹 消除,反映出持续的 疫苗接种 覆盖和强大的 监测 系统。然而,北美现在正在经历明显的复苏。在连续使用 传播 超过 12 个月后,加拿大于 2025 年 11 月 10 日正式失去 麻疹 消除地位,美国预计将于 2026 年 11 月失去其地位。流行病学趋势强调了逆转的严重程度。加拿大报告 2025 年报告了 5461 例 麻疹 病例,2026 年报告了 1121 例新增病例(截至 10 月 3 日),而 2024 年仅记录了 146 例。在美国,2025 年记录了 2288 例和 48 例 麻疹 病例,2026 年新增了 4410 例(截至 10 月 15 日),而 2024 年仅记录了 2288 例和 48 例 麻疹 病例,2026 年新增了 4410 例(截至 10 月 15 日)。2024 年将有 285 例病例和 16 例 暴发。值得注意的是,到 2025 年,美国 90% 的病例与 暴发 相关,93% 发生在未接种疫苗或状况不明的个体中,11% 需要住院治疗,2025 年记录了 3 例死亡——这是十年来的第一次。泛美卫生组织的区域数据显示,2025 年美洲地区有 14,503 例病例和 32 例死亡,2026 年有 55,792 例病例和 77 例死亡(截至 10 月 3 日)。麻疹 覆盖率下降是这种复苏的核心,美国幼儿园 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹覆盖率下降至 92.5%,低于估计支持 麻疹 人群免疫力的 95% 阈值。促成因素包括扩大非医疗豁免、入学要求执行不力、免疫基础设施分散、公共卫生系统资金不足,以及因错误信息而加剧的 疫苗 犹豫不决。恢复消除状态需要协调一致的政策和计划行动,包括加强 疫苗接种 的授权、改进 监测 和免疫登记、增强社区参与以及重建公众对 疫苗 的信任。不采取行动会带来持续的 传播、可预防的发病率和 死亡率,特别是对于美洲的儿童。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
美洲地区于 2016 年实现了 麻疹 消除,反映出持续的 疫苗接种 覆盖和强大的 监测 系统。然而,北美现在正在经历明显的复苏。
设计与对象
在连续超过 12 个月的 传播 后,加拿大于 2025 年 11 月 10 日正式失去 麻疹 消除地位,美国预计将于 2026 年 11 月失去其地位。
核心方法
流行病学趋势凸显了逆转的严重程度。泛美卫生组织的区域数据显示,2025 年美洲地区有 14,503 例病例和 32 例死亡,2026 年有 55,792 例病例和 77 例死亡(截至 10 月 3 日)。
主要结果
加拿大报告 2025 年报告了 5461 例 麻疹 病例,2026 年报告了 1121 例新增病例(截至 10 月 3 日),而 2024 年仅记录了 146 例。在美国,2025 年记录了 2288 例和 48 例 麻疹 病例,2026 年新增了 4410 例(截至 10 月 15 日),而 2024 年仅记录了 2288 例和 48 例 麻疹 病例,2026 年新增了 4410 例(截至 10 月 15 日)。2024 年将有 285 起案件和 16 起 暴发。
解释与创新
麻疹 覆盖率下降是这种复苏的核心,美国幼儿园 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹覆盖率下降至 92.5%,低于估计支持 麻疹 人群免疫力的 95% 阈值。促成因素包括扩大非医疗豁免、入学要求执行不力、免疫基础设施分散、公共卫生系统资金不足,以及因错误信息而加剧的 疫苗 犹豫不决。
科研与公卫意义
恢复消除状态需要协调一致的政策和计划行动,包括加强 疫苗接种 的授权、改进 监测 和免疫登记、增强社区参与以及重建公众对 疫苗 的信任。不采取行动会带来持续的 传播、可预防的发病率和 死亡率,特别是对于美洲的儿童。
局限与证据强度
值得注意的是,到 2025 年,美国 90% 的病例与 暴发 相关,93% 发生在未接种疫苗或状况不明的个体中,11% 需要住院治疗,2025 年记录了 3 例死亡——这是十年来的首次。提供的证据未报告具体的统计分析或置信区间。

North America's measles reversal: Canada has lost elimination status, and the United States is next.

Authors: Sten H. Vermund, Rumour P. Piepenbrink, Elizabeth M. Traverse
Original abstract (English)
The region of the Americas achieved measles elimination in 2016, reflecting sustained vaccination coverage and strong surveillance systems. However, North America is now experiencing a marked resurgence. Canada formally lost measles elimination status on November 10, 2025, following more than 12 months of continuous transmission, and the United States is projected to lose its status in November 2026. Epidemiologic trends underscore the magnitude of reversal. Canada reported 5461 measles cases in 2025 and 1121 additional cases in 2026 (as of October 3), compared with only 146 cases in 2024. In the United States, 2288 cases and 48 outbreaks were documented in 2025 and 4410 additional cases in 2026 (as of October 15), up from only 285 cases and 16 outbreaks in 2024. Notably, 90% of US cases were outbreak-associated in 2025, 93% occurred among unvaccinated or unknown-status individuals, 11% required hospitalization, and three deaths in 2025 were recorded - the first in a decade. Regional data from the Pan American Health Organization documented 14,503 cases and 32 deaths across the Americas in 2025, with 55,792 cases and 77 deaths in 2026 (as of October 3). Declining vaccination coverage is central to this resurgence, with US kindergarten measles-mumps-rubella coverage falling to 92.5%, below the 95% threshold estimated to support measles population immunity. Contributing factors include expansion of nonmedical exemptions, weak enforcement of school-entry requirements, fragmented immunization infrastructures, underfunded public health systems, and increasing vaccine hesitancy amplified by misinformation. Restoring elimination status will require coordinated policy and programmatic action, including strengthening vaccination mandates, improving surveillance and immunization registries, enhancing community engagement, and rebuilding public trust in vaccines. Failure to act risks sustained transmission, preventable morbidity, and mortality, particularly in children in the Americas.

The supplied evidence does not report specific statistical analyses or confidence intervals.

那他珠单抗治疗的多发性硬化症患者中 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 疫苗接种 的初步安全性观察:在意大利 麻疹 发病率 发病率上升背景下的病例系列。

Preliminary safety observations following measles-mumps-rubella vaccination in natalizumab-treated patients with multiple sclerosis: a case series in the context of rising measles incidence in Italy.
作者： Sticchi Laura, Postma Peter, Guarise Emanuele, Mikulska Malgorzata, Laroni Alice, Icardi Giancarlo, Inglese Matilde, Lapucci Caterina, Multiple Sclerosis Disease Management Team (MS DMT), E. Guarise
摘要中文翻译背景:在那他珠单抗(NTZ)治疗的患者中,只有灭活的 疫苗 被证明是安全的;直播疫苗需谨慎。意大利出现了 麻疹 疫情的死灰复燃,主要发生在患有明显并发症的未接种疫苗的人群中。疫苗 覆盖范围不足。目的:鉴于风险不断上升,我们的多发性硬化症疾病管理团队在 NTZ 治疗易感患者期间认可了 麻疹 疫苗接种,这与之前的选择性 VZV 疫苗接种 一致。结果:迄今为止,我们中心已有 11 名患者在 NTZ 治疗 期间接种了 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗 疫苗。在三种情况下,需要与 VZV 疫苗 共同给药。接受 疫苗 治疗的患者均未出现与 疫苗 相关的不良反应或 VZV 感染。结论:多发性硬化症的管理需要一种综合方法,平衡免疫抑制 治疗 风险与预防策略(例如 疫苗接种),以降低感染风险,同时确保患者安全。需要更大的患者 队列研究 来证实我们的初步发现。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于风险不断上升,我们的多发性硬化症疾病管理团队在 NTZ 治疗易感患者期间认可了 麻疹 疫苗接种,这与之前的选择性 VZV 疫苗接种 一致。
设计与对象
迄今为止,我们中心已有 11 名患者在 NTZ 治疗 期间接种了 麻疹-腮腺炎-风疹 (MMR) 疫苗 疫苗。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
接受 疫苗 治疗的患者均未出现与 疫苗 相关的不良反应或 VZV 感染。
解释与创新
MS 的管理需要一种综合方法,平衡免疫抑制 治疗 风险与预防策略(例如 疫苗接种),以降低感染风险,同时确保患者安全。
科研与公卫意义
需要更大的患者 队列研究 来证实我们的初步发现。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取基于检索到的全文证据。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Preliminary safety observations following measles-mumps-rubella vaccination in natalizumab-treated patients with multiple sclerosis: a case series in the context of rising measles incidence in Italy.

Authors: Sticchi Laura, Postma Peter, Guarise Emanuele, Mikulska Malgorzata, Laroni Alice, Icardi Giancarlo, Inglese Matilde, Lapucci Caterina, Multiple Sclerosis Disease Management Team (MS DMT), E. Guarise
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In Natalizumab (NTZ)-treated patients, only inactivated vaccines are proven safe; live vaccines require caution. Italy shows a measles resurgence, mainly in unvaccinated people with notable complications. Vaccine coverage is insufficient. OBJECTIVES: Given rising risk, our multiple sclerosis Disease Management Team endorsed measles vaccination during NTZ therapy for susceptible patients, consistent with prior selective VZV vaccination. RESULTS: To date, 11 patients have been vaccinated with the Measles-Mumps-Rubella (MMR) vaccine during NTZ treatment at our centre. In three cases, co-administration with the VZV vaccine was necessary. None of the patients who received the vaccine developed vaccine-associated adverse reactions or VZV infection. CONCLUSIONS: Management of MS requires an integrated approach that balances immunosuppressive treatment risks with preventive strategies, such as vaccination, to reduce infection risk while ensuring patient safety. Larger patient cohorts are needed to confirm our preliminary findings.

2016-2021 年莫桑比克国家基于病例的 麻疹 和风疹 IgM 阳性率以及病例分类准确性

Measles and rubella IgM positivity and case classification accuracy in Mozambique's national case-based surveillance system, 2016–2021
作者： Arlindo Raúl Muhôco, Catia Taibo, Cristolde Salomão, Liliana Dengo Baloi, Érika Rosseto, Cynthia Semá Baltazar, Roberta Horth, Liliana Dengo-Baloi, Rosseto É
摘要中文翻译摘要简介:莫桑比克于2016年针对麻疹和风疹采用了基于病例的监测。本研究旨在描述临床疑似病例的特征并评估血清学和流行病学数据的质量。方法 我们使用 2016-2021 年国家 监测 系统的数据进行了一项横断面研究。根据国家方案,所有临床疑似病例(发烧和全身皮疹)均需接受 麻疹 和风疹 IgM 检测。分析具有有效(充足样本和明确结果)麻疹 IgM 结果 (n = 12,674)、风疹 IgM 结果 (n = 12,177) 或两者 (n = 12,143) 的已用病例。多变量逻辑回归确定了与 IgM 阳性相关的因素。结果 在 15,782 例疑似病例中,12,708 例(81%)至少有一项有效 IgM 结果(麻疹 为 12,674 例,风疹为 12,177 例)。在接受这两种检测的人中 (n = 12,143),麻疹 IgM 呈阳性的人数为 1,581 人 (13%),风疹 IgM 呈阳性的人数为 993 人 (8.2%),两者均呈阳性的人数为 424 人 (3.5%)。在具有任何 IgM 结果的患者中 (n = 12,708),4,368 例 (34%) 在皮疹发病后的最佳采集窗口(麻疹 为 4-28 天;风疹为 6-28 天)采集了标本,597 例 (4.8%) 在样本采集前 28 天以内接受了含有 麻疹 的 疫苗。在确诊的 麻疹 病例 (n = 1,697) 中,有 424 例 (25%) 风疹 IgM 呈阳性;在确诊的风疹病例 (n = 996) 中,424 例 (43%) 也为 麻疹 IgM 阳性。各省份均一致报告病例并提交样本。北部和中部地区的阳性率高于南部地区,在样本采集前 8-28 天接种疫苗的人群中,以及在最佳窗口内采样的人群中,与皮疹发病后 ≤ 3 天以内采样的人群中,阳性率较高(麻疹:16% vs 12%;调整后的比值比 [AOR] = 1.2 [1.0-1.3])。结论 近期接种疫苗的个体中,高比例的共阳性病例以及 IgM 阳性率升高,可能反映了 疫苗 诱导的 IgM 或交叉反应性 血清学。如果不整合 疫苗接种 病史和流行病学背景,仅 IgM 血清学 不足以进行准确的病例分类。监测 系统实现了更高的文档完整性、适当的采样时间和一致的分类标准应用,可以更好地生成准确的 发病率 估计值并及时检测 暴发。

Measles and rubella IgM positivity and case classification accuracy in Mozambique's national case-based surveillance system, 2016–2021

Authors: Arlindo Raúl Muhôco, Catia Taibo, Cristolde Salomão, Liliana Dengo Baloi, Érika Rosseto, Cynthia Semá Baltazar, Roberta Horth, Liliana Dengo-Baloi, Rosseto É
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction: Mozambique adopted case-based surveillance for measles and rubella in 2016. This study aimed to characterise clinically suspected cases and assess serological and epidemiological data quality. Methods We conducted a cross-sectional study using data from the national surveillance system, 2016–2021. Per national protocol, all clinically suspected cases (fever and generalised rash) are tested for measles and rubella IgM. Analyses used cases with valid (adequate specimen and clear result) measles IgM results (n = 12,674), rubella IgM results (n = 12,177), or both (n = 12,143). Multivariable logistic regression identified factors associated with IgM positivity. Results Of 15,782 suspected cases, 12,708 (81%) had at least one valid IgM result (12,674 for measles and 12,177 for rubella). Among those tested for both (n = 12,143), IgM was positive for measles in 1,581 (13%), for rubella in 993 (8.2%), and for both in 424 (3.5%). Among those with any IgM result (n = 12,708), 4,368 (34%) had specimens collected in the optimal collection window (4–28 days for measles; 6–28 days for rubella) after rash onset, and 597 (4.8%) had received measles-containing vaccine ≤ 28 days before sample collection. Among confirmed measles cases (n = 1,697), 424 (25%) were also rubella IgM-positive; among confirmed rubella cases (n = 996), 424 (43%) were also measles IgM-positive. All provinces consistently reported cases and submitted samples. Positivity was higher in the northern and central regions versus the southern region, among those vaccinated 8–28 days before sample collection, and among those sampled in the optimal window versus ≤ 3 days after rash onset (measles: 16% vs 12%; adjusted odds ratio [AOR] = 1.2 [1.0–1.3]). Conclusion The high proportion of co-positive cases, together with elevated IgM positivity among recently vaccinated individuals, may reflect vaccine-induced IgM or cross-reactive serology. IgM serology alone is insufficient for accurate case classification without integration of vaccination history and epidemiological context. Surveillance systems that achieve higher completeness of documentation, appropriate sample timing, and consistent application of classification criteria are better positioned to generate accurate incidence estimates and detect outbreaks promptly.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterise clinically suspected cases and assess serological and epidemiological data quality. Mozambique adopted case-based surveillance for measles and rubella in 2016.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted using data from the national surveillance system, 2016–2021. Analyses used cases with valid measles IgM results (n = 12,674), rubella IgM results (n = 12,177), or both (n = 12,143).
Core methods
Multivariable logistic regression identified factors associated with IgM positivity. Per national protocol, all clinically suspected cases (fever and generalised rash) are tested for measles and rubella IgM.
Main results
Of 15,782 suspected cases, 12,708 (81%) had at least one valid IgM result (12,674 for measles and 12,177 for rubella). Among those tested for both (n = 12,143), IgM was positive for measles in 1,581 (13%), for rubella in 993 (8.2%), and for both in 424 (3.5%).
Interpretation and novelty
The high proportion of co-positive cases, together with elevated IgM positivity among recently vaccinated individuals, may reflect vaccine-induced IgM or cross-reactive serology.
Scientific and public-health significance
IgM serology alone is insufficient for accurate case classification without integration of vaccination history and epidemiological context. Surveillance systems that achieve higher completeness of documentation, appropriate sample timing, and consistent application of classification criteria are better positioned to generate accurate incidence estimates and detect outbreaks promptly.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design. Confidence in the findings is high based on the comprehensive data collection and statistical analysis.

亚急性硬化性全脑炎 患者的弥散张量成像:一项前瞻性随访研究。

Diffusion Tensor Imaging in Patients with Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis: a Prospective Follow-up Study.
作者： Keerthiraj Db, Shweta Pandey, Ravindra Kumar Garg, Hardeep Singh Malhotra, Rajesh Verma, Durgesh Kumar Dwivedi, Anit Parihar, Abdul Qavi, Neeraj Kumar, Ravi Uniyal
摘要中文翻译目的:传统 MRI 可能低估 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 的疾病负担。临床疾病阶段和常规神经影像学通常不相关。我们评估了各个临床阶段的弥散张量成像(DTI)异常及其在六个月内的纵向变化。目的:评估 SSPE 临床阶段的 DTI 异常,并评估六个月内分数各向异性 (FA) 和平均扩散率 (MD) 的纵向变化。方法:在这项前瞻性研究中,41 名 SSPE 患者(II 期:n = 15;III 期:n = 26)和 15 名年龄匹配的健康对照接受了 MRI 和 DTI 扫描。我们测量了多个预定义大脑区域和束的分数各向异性 (FA) 和平均扩散率 (MD)。使用 Kruskal-Wallis 检验评估各组 FA 和 MD 值的差异,然后使用成对 Mann-Whitney U 检验进行组间比较。对于成对比较,组间差异的大小表示为具有相应 95% 置信区间 (CI) 的平均差异。使用 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验评估同一患者 FA 和 MD 值从基线到 6 个月随访的纵向变化。为了解释预定义大脑区域和白质束之间的多重比较,使用 Benjamini-Hochberg 错误发现率 (FDR) 程序调整 p 值,并报告 FDR 调整后的 q 值。双边 p 值或 FDR 调整的 q 值 < 0.05 被认为具有统计显着性。结果:与健康对照相比,SSPE 患者表现出 FA 减少和 MD 增加,并且 III 期疾病有更广泛的异常。即使在常规 MRI 正常的患者中也能检测到 DTI 异常。六个月时,大多数检查区域的 FA 下降,MD 增加,表明尽管存在 治疗,但白质完整性逐渐丧失。在个体内分析中,胼胝体压部表现出相对一致的 FA 减少和 MD 增加,而枕叶和顶叶白质显示出更大的个体间异质性,尽管群体水平存在显着变化。结论:DTI 显示 SSPE 中进行性白质异常,而传统 MRI 可能无法完全捕获这些异常。胼胝体压部相对一致的纵向 FA 和 MD 变化表明,其可作为监测疾病进展的影像生物标志物;然而,需要在更大的纵向 队列研究 中进行验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
传统 MRI 可能低估 亚急性硬化性全脑炎 (SSPE) 的疾病负担。临床疾病阶段和常规神经影像学通常不相关。
设计与对象
在这项前瞻性研究中,41 名 SSPE 患者(II 期:n = 15;III 期:n = 26)和 15 名年龄匹配的健康对照接受了弥散张量成像 (DTI) MRI。
核心方法
我们测量了多个预定义大脑区域和束的分数各向异性 (FA) 和平均扩散率 (MD)。使用 Kruskal-Wallis 检验评估各组 FA 和 MD 值的差异,然后使用成对 Mann-Whitney U 检验进行组间比较。对于成对比较,组间差异的大小表示为具有相应 95% 置信区间 (CI) 的平均差异。使用 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验评估同一患者 FA 和 MD 值从基线到 6 个月随访的纵向变化。
主要结果
与健康对照相比,SSPE 患者的 FA 减少,MD 增加,III 期疾病出现更广泛的异常。即使在常规 MRI 正常的患者中也能检测到 DTI 异常。六个月时,大多数检查区域的 FA 下降,MD 增加,表明尽管存在 治疗,但白质完整性逐渐丧失。
解释与创新
DTI 显示 SSPE 中进行性白质异常,传统 MRI 可能无法完全捕获这些异常。胼胝体压部相对一致的纵向 FA 和 MD 变化表明它可以作为监测疾病进展的成像生物标志物。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,弥散张量成像 (DTI) 可以改善 SSPE 中白质异常的检测,而传统 MRI 可能无法完全捕获这种异常。这可以为 监测 和疾病进展监测提供更准确的信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量相对较小和使用单一中心的限制。该证据是中等的,因为它基于一项前瞻性随访研究和统计分析。

Diffusion Tensor Imaging in Patients with Subacute Sclerosing Panencephalitis: a Prospective Follow-up Study.

Authors: Keerthiraj Db, Shweta Pandey, Ravindra Kumar Garg, Hardeep Singh Malhotra, Rajesh Verma, Durgesh Kumar Dwivedi, Anit Parihar, Abdul Qavi, Neeraj Kumar, Ravi Uniyal
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Conventional MRI may underestimate disease burden in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE). Clinical disease stage and conventional neuroimaging often do not correlate. We evaluated diffusion tensor imaging (DTI) abnormalities across clinical stages and their longitudinal changes over six months. OBJECTIVE: To evaluate DTI abnormalities across clinical stages of SSPE and assess longitudinal changes in fractional anisotropy (FA) and mean diffusivity (MD) over six months. METHODS: In this prospective study, 41 patients with SSPE (Stage II: n = 15; Stage III: n = 26) and 15 age-matched healthy controls underwent MRI with DTI. We measured fractional anisotropy (FA) and mean diffusivity (MD) across multiple predefined brain regions and tracts. Differences in FA and MD values across groups were assessed using the Kruskal-Wallis test, followed by pairwise Mann-Whitney U tests for between-group comparisons. For pairwise comparisons, the magnitude of between-group differences was expressed as the mean difference with corresponding 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Longitudinal changes in FA and MD values from baseline to the 6‐month follow-up within the same patients were assessed using the Wilcoxon signed-rank test. To account for multiple comparisons across the predefined brain regions and white matter tracts, p-values were adjusted using the Benjamini-Hochberg false discovery rate (FDR) procedure, and FDR-adjusted q‐values were reported. A two-sided p-value or FDR-adjusted q‐value < 0.05 was considered statistically significant. RESULTS: SSPE patients demonstrated reduced FA and increased MD compared with healthy controls, with more extensive abnormalities in Stage III disease. DTI abnormalities were detected even in patients with normal conventional MRI. At six months, FA decreased and MD increased in most examined regions, indicating progressive loss of white-matter integrity despite treatment. In intraindividual analysis, the splenium of the corpus callosum demonstrated relatively consistent FA reduction and MD increase, whereas the occipital and parietal white matter showed greater interindividual heterogeneity despite significant group-level changes. CONCLUSION: DTI demonstrates progressive white-matter abnormalities in SSPE that may not be fully captured by conventional MRI. The relatively consistent longitudinal FA and MD changes in the splenium of the corpus callosum suggest that it may serve as an imaging biomarker for monitoring disease progression; however, its validation in larger longitudinal cohorts is needed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Conventional MRI may underestimate disease burden in subacute sclerosing panencephalitis (SSPE). Clinical disease stage and conventional neuroimaging often do not correlate.
Design and population
In this prospective study, 41 patients with SSPE (Stage II: n = 15; Stage III: n = 26) and 15 age-matched healthy controls underwent MRI with diffusion tensor imaging (DTI).
Core methods
We measured fractional anisotropy (FA) and mean diffusivity (MD) across multiple predefined brain regions and tracts. Differences in FA and MD values across groups were assessed using the Kruskal-Wallis test, followed by pairwise Mann-Whitney U tests for between-group comparisons. For pairwise comparisons, the magnitude of between-group differences was expressed as the mean difference with corresponding 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Longitudinal changes in FA and MD values from baseline to the 6‐month follow-up within the same patients were assessed using the Wilcoxon signed-rank test.
Main results
SSPE patients demonstrated reduced FA and increased MD compared with healthy controls, with more extensive abnormalities in Stage III disease. DTI abnormalities were detected even in patients with normal conventional MRI. At six months, FA decreased and MD increased in most examined regions, indicating progressive loss of white-matter integrity despite treatment.
Interpretation and novelty
DTI demonstrates progressive white-matter abnormalities in SSPE that may not be fully captured by conventional MRI. The relatively consistent longitudinal FA and MD changes in the splenium of the corpus callosum suggest that it may serve as an imaging biomarker for monitoring disease progression.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that diffusion tensor imaging (DTI) could improve the detection of white-matter abnormalities in SSPE, which may not be fully captured by conventional MRI. This could inform more accurate surveillance and monitoring of disease progression.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its relatively small sample size and the use of a single center. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a prospective follow-up study with statistical analysis.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

幼儿园 麻疹 麻疹 覆盖率和 2025-2026 年 麻疹 在美国的复苏:对公开 监测 数据的州级分析

Kindergarten measles vaccination coverage and the 2025–2026 measles resurgence in the United States: a state-level analysis of public surveillance data
作者： Shrreya Jain
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定各州幼儿园 MMR 麻疹 覆盖率较低是否与 2026 年 麻疹 消除状态丧失后 2025 年至 2026 年期间更多 麻疹 病例复苏相关。
设计与对象
使用 2009-10 至 2025-26 年间幼儿园 MMR 覆盖率和非医疗豁免数据,结合州和县 麻疹 病例数、人口普查人口估计以及加拿大和墨西哥的国家数据进行了一项生态研究。
核心方法
使用 Spearman 相关性和负二项式回归,以及 MMR 覆盖率和非医疗豁免、麻疹 病例和人口估计数据来评估关联性。
主要结果
National MMR coverage fell from 95.2% in 2019–20 to 92.4% in 2025–26, and non-medical exemptions increased from 2.2% to 4.0%. Each 1-percentage-point decrease in coverage was associated with a 15% increase in cases (incidence rate ratio 1.15, 95% CI 1.01–1.31).
解释与创新
研究发现,较低的 MMR 覆盖率与更多的 麻疹 病例相关,并且该覆盖率并不能预测较大的 麻疹 病例。研究结果表明,要重新消除 麻疹,必须将覆盖率恢复到 95% 以上。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为公共卫生策略提供信息,以提高 疫苗接种 覆盖率并预防未来的 麻疹 暴发,特别是在覆盖率较低的州。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到生态设计的限制,无法建立因果关系,并且受到关联对排除单一状态的敏感性的限制。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Kindergarten measles vaccination coverage and the 2025–2026 measles resurgence in the United States: a state-level analysis of public surveillance data

Authors: Shrreya Jain
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background In November 2026, the United States lost the measles elimination status it had held since 2000. This study examined whether states with lower kindergarten measles, mumps and rubella (MMR) vaccination coverage had more measles during the 2025–2026 resurgence, and how coverage changed before, during and after the COVID-19 pandemic. Methods In this ecological study, kindergarten MMR coverage and non-medical exemptions (CDC SchoolVaxView, 2009–10 to 2025–26) were combined with state and county measles case counts, census population estimates and national data for Canada and Mexico. Associations were assessed with Spearman correlation and negative binomial regression. Results National coverage fell from 95.2% in 2019–20 to 92.4% in 2025–26, and non-medical exemptions rose from 2.2% to 4.0%. Coverage dropped in most states during the pandemic and did not recover afterward. Measles cases averaged 410 per year before the pandemic, 61 during it and 2,011 after it. Each 1-percentage-point decrease in coverage was associated with 15% more cases (incidence rate ratio 1.15, 95% CI 1.01–1.31), although the association was sensitive to the exclusion of single states and coverage did not predict large outbreaks. Of 8,025 cases with county data, 96.8% were locally acquired, and half occurred in 18 counties or health regions. Conclusions Kindergarten MMR coverage fell during the pandemic and has not recovered. States with lower coverage had more measles, and cases were concentrated in a few counties. Restoring coverage above 95% is needed to re-establish elimination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine if lower kindergarten MMR vaccination coverage in states was associated with more measles cases during the 2025–2026 resurgence, following the loss of measles elimination status in 2026.
Design and population
An ecological study was conducted using kindergarten MMR coverage and non-medical exemptions data from 2009–10 to 2025–26, combined with state and county measles case counts, census population estimates, and national data from Canada and Mexico.
Core methods
Associations were assessed using Spearman correlation and negative binomial regression, with data on MMR coverage and non-medical exemptions, measles cases, and population estimates.
Main results
National MMR coverage fell from 95.2% in 2019–20 to 92.4% in 2025–26, and non-medical exemptions increased from 2.2% to 4.0%. Each 1-percentage-point decrease in coverage was associated with a 15% increase in cases (incidence rate ratio 1.15, 95% CI 1.01–1.31).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that lower MMR coverage was associated with more measles cases, and that coverage did not predict large outbreaks. The findings suggest that restoring coverage above 95% is necessary to re-establish measles elimination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies to improve vaccination coverage and prevent future measles outbreaks, particularly in states with lower coverage.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the ecological design, which cannot establish causality, and by the sensitivity of the association to the exclusion of single states. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

Divergent estimates of subacute sclerosing panencephalitis risk after measles and the projected burden of the 2023–2026 resurgence: a Bayesian modelling study
作者： Tebyan Omer

Divergent estimates of subacute sclerosing panencephalitis risk after measles and the projected burden of the 2023–2026 resurgence: a Bayesian modelling study

Authors: Tebyan Omer
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Published estimates of the risk of subacute sclerosing panencephalitis after measles differ more than tenfold, and the burden of the 2023–2026 measles resurgence is unknown. We examined why and projected that burden. Methods Nine populations with counts of subacute sclerosing panencephalitis and measles denominators were identified from the literature, and a structured search of three databases checked completeness. Counts were expressed as observed/expected ratios under the 1991 England and Wales reference model. Bayesian hierarchical Poisson models compared study design and the way measles infections were counted (a covariate specified after earlier analyses) as explanations for differences between populations, assessed by leave-one-out cross-validation, permutation tests and 30 prespecified sensitivity analyses. Risk per infection was then applied to infections implied by measles cases reported to the World Health Organization from January 2023 to mid-October 2026. Results The populations contributed 499 cases; observed/expected ratios ranged from 0.33 to 8.14. The three populations whose denominators estimated all infections had ratios of 1.6 or less, whereas five of six with denominators counting notifications or clinic visits had ratios of about 4 to 8 (ratio 6.7, 95% credible interval 2.1–19.3). Study design fitted about as well (ratio 5.5, 1.6–16.9); the two explanations could not be separated. Relative to older ages, risk after infection in infancy was about twice that assumed by the reference (multiplier 2.1, 1.2–3.5), although the interval included 1 when either of two strata was omitted. The reported cases correspond to about 40 million infections, which are projected to cause a median of 1,564 (95% interval 254–10,376) to 2,601 (379–18,509) cases depending on the explanation, or about half if scaled to officially reported cases. More than 90% of onsets will occur after 2026, peaking around 2030. Conclusions How measles infections were counted may explain much of this variation but cannot be separated from study design; published risks should not be transferred between settings without accounting for the denominator. Under either explanation, the 2023–2026 resurgence is projected to cause hundreds to thousands of cases, mostly during 2027–2035, leaving time to strengthen surveillance and protect infants.

具有不完整 疫苗接种 和 1 个免疫力减弱的 麻疹 的 SVIR 模型分析:Sidoarjo-Indonesia 案例

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case
作者： Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
摘要中文翻译麻疹仍然是印度尼西亚的重大公共卫生挑战,尤其是在疫苗接种覆盖率异质性和免疫力下降的地区。本研究开发并分析了一个针对麻疹传播的易感-接种-感染-康复(SVIR)分组模型,该模型纳入了免疫力下降的因素。基本再生数R0是通过下一代矩阵方法推导的,并且通过李雅普诺夫函数严格证明了无病平衡态和地方性平衡态的全局稳定性。模型参数是从2023年Sidoarjo市每周的监测数据中使用粒子群优化(PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)进行评估。PSO方法实现了最低的MAPE(11.17%),优于其他方法。估计的R0 = 0.668确认了无病状态。归一化敏感性分析确定疫苗效力是最关键的参数;疫苗效力下降至0 .75会使R0接近临界阈值,任何进一步的减少都会使系统进入地方性状态。这些发现强调了通过加强剂接种计划和强化疫苗接种策略保持高疫苗效力的必要性,以在区级维持麻疹的消除。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
使用下一代矩阵方法推导出基本繁殖数R0,并通过Lyapunov函数严格证明了无病平衡和地方病平衡的全局稳定性。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
本研究开发并分析了印度尼西亚诗都阿佐市麻疹 传播 的易感-疫苗接种-感染-康复 (SVIR) 区室模型,其中考虑了免疫力的减弱。
主要结果
模型参数是根据西多阿佐市 2023 年每周的 监测 数据使用粒子群优化 (PSO)、扩展卡尔曼滤波器、最大似然估计和最小二乘法进行估计,并通过平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 进行评估。PSO 实现了最低的 MAPE (11.17%),优于其他方法。
解释与创新
估计的 R0 = 0.668 确认无病状态。
科研与公卫意义
麻疹 在印度尼西亚仍然是一个重大的公共卫生挑战,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖范围不均匀且免疫力下降的地区。
局限与证据强度
Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

SVIR Model Analysis of Measles with Incomplete Vaccination and 1 Waning Immunity: Sidoarjo-Indonesia Case

Authors: Ainishafa Ainishafa, Abadi Abadi, Dian Savitri
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health challenge in Indonesia, especially in regionswith heterogeneous vaccination coverage and waning immunity. This study develops andanalyzes a Susceptible–Vaccinated–Infected–Recovered (SVIR) compartmental model formeasles transmission in Sidoarjo City, Indonesia, incorporating waning immunity. The basicreproduction number R0 is derived using the next-generation matrix method, and the globalstability of both the disease-free and endemic equilibria is rigorously proven via Lyapunovfunctions. Model parameters are estimated from weekly 2023 surveillance data in Sidoarjo Cityusing Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO), Extended Kalman Filter, Maximum LikelihoodEstimation, and Least Squares, and evaluated via Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE).PSO achieves the lowest MAPE (11.17%), outperforming the other methods. The estimatedR0 = 0.668confirms a disease-free regime. Normalized sensitivity analysis identifies vaccineefficacy as the most influential parameter; a decline in efficacy to0 .75elevates R0 close tothe critical threshold, and any further reduction pushes the system into an endemic state.These findings underscore the necessity of maintaining high vaccine efficacy through boosterprograms and strengthened immunization strategies to sustain measles elimination at thedistrict level.

Risk factors associated with the resurgence of confirmed measles cases in Senegal in the post Covid-19 period
作者： Boly Diop, Abdourahmane Ndao, Cheikh Amadou Bamba Dath, Mouhamadou Falilou TINE, Ibrahima NDIAYE, Jean Augustin Diégane Tine, Mayacine Diongue, Oumar Bassoum, Erick Lukusa Mbuyamba, Mamadou Ndiaye

Risk factors associated with the resurgence of confirmed measles cases in Senegal in the post Covid-19 period

Authors: Boly Diop, Abdourahmane Ndao, Cheikh Amadou Bamba Dath, Mouhamadou Falilou TINE, Ibrahima NDIAYE, Jean Augustin Diégane Tine, Mayacine Diongue, Oumar Bassoum, Erick Lukusa Mbuyamba, Mamadou Ndiaye
Original abstract (English)
Measles is one of the most contagious infectious diseases, responsible for significant morbidity and mortality through recurring epidemics. In Senegal, a resurgence of confirmed measles cases was observed, with epidemics occurring between 2021 and 2024. The goal of this study is to identify factors significantly associated with laboratory-confirmed status among notified measles cases in Senegal in the post-Covid-19 context. A cross-sectional analytical study was conducted on all suspected measles cases notified in the health districts of Senegal from January 2015 to December 2024 ( n = 9 935), each classified as confirmed, doubtful, or ruled out. Confirmed cases were described separately ( n = 2 448). Statistical analyses were performed using R software with Cramér’s V, Multiple Correspondence Analysis, and multivariable binary logistic regression comparing confirmed cases with doubtful/ruled-out cases. Among 9 935 suspected measles cases notified over the study period, 2 448 were laboratory-confirmed. Patients ranged in age from 1 to 81 years, with a mean age of 9 years. Several factors were associated with laboratory-confirmed status. The Kédougou region (OR: 5, 95% CI [3.71–6.78], p < 2.2e- 16 ) was associated with a fivefold increased risk of a confirmed measles case. Insufficient vaccination coverage for the second dose (RR2) was strongly linked to confirmed status (OR: 3.34, 95% CI [2.83–3.95], p < 2.2e- 16 ). The risk was also four times higher in unvaccinated individuals (OR: 4.03, 95% CI [3.54–4.6], p < 2.2e- 16 ). Finally, children aged 1 to 5 years (OR: 1.39, 95% CI [1.24–1.55], p = 0.0003593) appear to be a particularly vulnerable age group, with an increased risk compared to other age groups. This study identified and ranked the principal risk factors associated with the resurgence of confirmed measles cases in Senegal. These findings could inform future research exploring machine-learning-based approaches to outbreak risk prediction; however, the development and validation of any such decision-support tool remain beyond the scope of the present analysis.

基于废水的 监测 作为以色列疫苗接种不足社区中 麻疹 暴发 检测的补充工具

Wastewater-Based Surveillance as a Complementary Tool for Measles Outbreak Detection in Under-Vaccinated Communities in Israel
作者： Lihi Gershon, Efrat Dahan Bucris, Liora Guy David, Hilla De-Leon, Irina Aguvaev, Tal Butensky, Daniel Avhar, Rona Rotaro, Yaniv Lustig, Oran Erster
摘要中文翻译麻疹 暴发 在全球范围内重新出现,特别是在 麻疹 覆盖率不佳的社区中,这凸显了对补充 监测 方法的需求。我们评估了 2025 年以色列基于废水的 麻疹病毒 (WBE) 作为检测和监测 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 暴发 的工具。2025 年 6 月至 12 月期间,每周从七个 监测 集水区收集废水样本,使用膜过滤进行浓缩,并通过数字 PCR (dPCR) 进行分析。将环境调查结果与分配到相应废水集水区的实验室确诊的 麻疹 病例进行比较。MeV RNA 仅在七个监测地点中的两个(Beit Shemesh 和 Safed)中检测到,这两个地点均服务于 疫苗接种 覆盖率较低(<82%)的社区。在这些流域中,废水 MeV RNA 浓度与实验室确诊病例的动态密切相关,而尽管连续 监测,但在其余 5 个地点并未检测到 MeV RNA。在 Safed,废水 MeV RNA 浓度的增加比实验室确诊病例的增加早了大约一周,这证明了 WBE 作为预警系统的潜力。我们的研究结果表明,废水麻疹是一种有价值的流行病学工具,可以补充传统的监测,并有可能提高麻疹 暴发的态势感知,协助公共卫生决策,并促进对弱势群体的有针对性的干预。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
我们评估了基于废水的 流行病学 (WBE) 作为 2025 年在以色列检测和监测 麻疹病毒 (MeV) 暴发 的工具。
设计与对象
将环境调查结果与分配到相应废水集水区的实验室确诊的 麻疹 病例进行比较。
核心方法
2025 年 6 月至 12 月期间,每周从七个 监测 集水区收集废水样本,使用膜过滤进行浓缩,并通过数字 PCR (dPCR) 进行分析。
主要结果
MeV RNA 仅在七个监测地点中的两个(Beit Shemesh 和 Safed)中检测到,这两个地点均服务于 疫苗接种 覆盖率较低(<82%)的社区。
解释与创新
在这些流域中,废水 MeV RNA 浓度与实验室确诊病例的动态密切相关,而尽管连续 监测,但在其余 5 个地点并未检测到 MeV RNA。
科研与公卫意义
我们的研究结果表明,废水麻疹是一种有价值的流行病学工具,可以补充传统的监测,并有可能提高麻疹 暴发的态势感知,协助公共卫生决策,并促进对弱势群体的有针对性的干预。
局限与证据强度
在 Safed,废水 MeV RNA 浓度的增加比实验室确诊病例的增加早了大约一周,这证明了 WBE 作为预警系统的潜力。

Wastewater-Based Surveillance as a Complementary Tool for Measles Outbreak Detection in Under-Vaccinated Communities in Israel

Authors: Lihi Gershon, Efrat Dahan Bucris, Liora Guy David, Hilla De-Leon, Irina Aguvaev, Tal Butensky, Daniel Avhar, Rona Rotaro, Yaniv Lustig, Oran Erster
Original abstract (English)
Measles outbreaks have re-emerged globally, particularly in communities with suboptimal vaccination coverage, highlighting the need for complementary surveillance approaches. We evaluated wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) as a tool for detecting and monitoring measles virus (MeV) outbreaks in Israel during 2025. Weekly wastewater samples were collected from seven surveillance catchments between June and December 2025, concentrated using membrane filtration, and analyzed by digital PCR (dPCR). Environmental findings were compared with laboratory-confirmed measles cases assigned to the corresponding wastewater catchments. MeV RNA was detected in only two of the seven monitored sites, Beit Shemesh and Safed, both serving communities with low vaccination coverage (<82%). In these catchments, wastewater MeV RNA concentrations closely mirrored the dynamics of laboratory-confirmed cases, while no MeV RNA was detected in the remaining five sites despite continuous surveillance. In Safed, increases in wastewater MeV RNA concentrations preceded the rise in laboratory-confirmed cases by approximately one week, demonstrating the potential of WBE as an early-warning system. Our findings demonstrate that wastewater surveillance is a valuable epidemiological tool that complements conventional surveillance and has the potential to improve situational awareness of measles outbreaks, assist public health decision-making, and facilitate targeted interventions in vulnerable populations.
View research seven-element analysis

麻疹 传播 模型中 疫苗接种 和 治疗 的自适应滑模控制

Adaptive Sliding Mode Control for Vaccination and Treatment in a Measles Transmission Model
作者： Dewi Suhika, Rifky Fauzi, Nela Rizka, Achmad Suryadi Nasution, Dear Michiko Mutiara Noor, Mira Mustika, Werry Febrianti, Lutfi Mardianto
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是一种高度传染性的 疫苗 可预防疾病,持续的 麻疹 凸显了将预防和 治疗 相结合的有效干预策略的必要性。
设计与对象
本研究开发了一个扩展的 麻疹 传播 模型,由易感人群、疫苗接种人群、暴露人群、感染人群、住院人群和康复人群组成,具有两个时间依赖性控制输入,分别代表 疫苗接种 和 治疗。
核心方法
提出了自适应滑模控制(ASMC)策略来将疫苗接种人群调节到规定的轨迹,同时抑制感染人群。针对疫苗接种和感染状态构建归一化滑动表面,并引入结合死区机制的自适应增益以防止不必要的增益增长。
主要结果
Numerical simulations are conducted over 200 weeks with an initial population of 10 million individuals, comparing the proposed ASMC strategy with an uncontrolled scenario under the same initial conditions and epidemiological parameters. The uncontrolled infectious population reaches a peak of approximately (364,005) individuals, whereas under ASMC it does not exceed its initial value of (75,900), corresponding to a (79.15%) reduction in the peak infectious population relative to the uncontrolled scenario. At the end of the simulation, the vaccinated population reaches approximately (4.61x10^6) individuals, representing (46.09%) of the modeled population. Moreover, the control inputs and adaptive gains remain bounded throughout the numerical simulation.
解释与创新
这些发现说明了所提出的 ASMC 框架在协调 疫苗接种 和 治疗 干预措施以及减少模拟峰值感染人群方面的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,所提出的 ASMC 框架可以有效减少感染人群高峰,这可以为管理 麻疹 暴发 的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数学模型,并未提供现实世界干预的经验证据。ASMC策略在现实场景中的有效性仍有待验证。

Adaptive Sliding Mode Control for Vaccination and Treatment in a Measles Transmission Model

Authors: Dewi Suhika, Rifky Fauzi, Nela Rizka, Achmad Suryadi Nasution, Dear Michiko Mutiara Noor, Mira Mustika, Werry Febrianti, Lutfi Mardianto
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a highly contagious vaccine-preventable disease, and persistent outbreaks highlight the need for effective intervention strategies combining prevention and treatment. This study develops an extended measles transmission model consisting of susceptible, vaccinated, exposed, infectious, hospitalized, and recovered populations, with two time-dependent control inputs representing vaccination and treatment. An Adaptive Sliding Mode Control (ASMC) strategy is proposed to regulate the vaccinated population toward a prescribed trajectory while suppressing the infectious population. Normalized sliding surfaces are constructed for the vaccinated and infectious states, and adaptive gains incorporating a dead-zone mechanism are introduced to prevent unnecessary gain growth. The positivity and boundedness of the epidemiological model solutions are established, while Lyapunov analysis demonstrates exponential convergence of the ideal sliding dynamics when the control saturation constraints are inactive. Numerical simulations are conducted over 200 weeks with an initial population of 10 million individuals, comparing the proposed ASMC strategy with an uncontrolled scenario under the same initial conditions and epidemiological parameters. The uncontrolled infectious population reaches a peak of approximately (364,005) individuals, whereas under ASMC it does not exceed its initial value of (75,900), corresponding to a (79.15%) reduction in the peak infectious population relative to the uncontrolled scenario. At the end of the simulation, the vaccinated population reaches approximately (4.61x10^6) individuals, representing (46.09%) of the modeled population. Moreover, the control inputs and adaptive gains remain bounded throughout the numerical simulation. These findings illustrate the potential of the proposed ASMC framework for coordinating vaccination and treatment interventions and reducing the simulated peak infectious population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a highly contagious vaccine-preventable disease, and persistent outbreaks highlight the need for effective intervention strategies combining prevention and treatment.
Design and population
This study develops an extended measles transmission model consisting of susceptible, vaccinated, exposed, infectious, hospitalized, and recovered populations, with two time-dependent control inputs representing vaccination and treatment.

Main results

The uncontrolled infectious population reaches a peak of approximately (364,005) individuals, whereas under ASMC it does not exceed its initial value of (75,900), corresponding to a (79.15%) reduction in the peak infectious population relative to the uncontrolled scenario. At the end of the simulation, the vaccinated population reaches approximately (4.61x10^6) individuals, representing (46.09%) of the modeled population. Moreover, the control inputs and adaptive gains remain bounded throughout the numerical simulation.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings illustrate the potential of the proposed ASMC framework for coordinating vaccination and treatment interventions and reducing the simulated peak infectious population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the proposed ASMC framework can effectively reduce the peak infectious population, which could inform strategies for managing measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a mathematical model and does not provide empirical evidence from real-world interventions. The effectiveness of the ASMC strategy in real-world scenarios remains to be validated.

用于检测和区分野生型和 疫苗 菌株 麻疹病毒 v1 的数字 PCR 三重检测

Digital PCR Triplex Assay for the Detection and Differentiation of Wild-Type and Vaccine-Strain Measles Viruses v1
作者： Victor Vusi Mabasa, Sipho Gwala, Kerrigan McCarthy, Mukhlid Yousif
摘要中文翻译该协议描述了一种优化的三重数字 PCR (dPCR) 测定,用于检测废水中的 麻疹病毒 RNA 以及区分野生型和 疫苗 菌株测量值 麻疹病毒。该测定在 QIAGEN QIAcuity 数字 PCR 仪器上的多重反应中使用带有水解探针的 QIAcuity OneStep Advanced Probe Kit。该方案还提供了内部 20× 引物-探针检测混合物的制备说明。开发三重检测的目的是通过将多个检测目标纳入单个反应中来提高废水中 麻疹病毒 监测 的灵敏度。与使用单独的检测相比,其实施已证明 麻疹病毒 总体检测率(阳性率)有所提高。尽管该测定旨在区分野生型和 疫苗 菌株 麻疹病毒,但在没有明确菌株分化的情况下成功检测 麻疹病毒 RNA 仍应被视为有价值的 麻疹病毒 结果。在这种情况下,样品应报告为 麻疹病毒 阳性,菌株分化记录为不确定。如果需要明确的菌株表征,建议使用全 基因组 测序 (WGS)。互补的 麻疹病毒 全 麻疹病毒 测序方案提供了对废水中检测到的 麻疹病毒 进行测序的详细说明,从而实现了进一步的遗传表征和菌株鉴定。

Digital PCR Triplex Assay for the Detection and Differentiation of Wild-Type and Vaccine-Strain Measles Viruses v1

Authors: Victor Vusi Mabasa, Sipho Gwala, Kerrigan McCarthy, Mukhlid Yousif
Original abstract (English)
This protocol describes an optimised triplex digital PCR (dPCR) assay for the detection of measles virus RNA in wastewater and the differentiation of wild-type and vaccine-strain meas measles virus. The assay uses the QIAcuity OneStep Advanced Probe Kit with hydrolysis probes in multiplex reactions on QIAGEN QIAcuity digital PCR instruments. The protocol also provides instructions for the preparation of in-house 20× primer–probe assay mixes. The triplex assay was developed to improve the sensitivity of measles virus surveillance in wastewater by incorporating multiple detection targets into a single reaction. Its implementation has demonstrated an improvement in the overall measles virus detection rate (positivity rate) compared with the use of individual assays. Although the assay is designed to differentiate between wild-type and vaccine-strain measles virus, successful detection of measles virus RNA without definitive strain differentiation should still be considered a valuable surveillance outcome. In such cases, the sample should be reported as measles virus-positive, with strain differentiation recorded as inconclusive. Where definitive strain characterisation is required, whole-genome sequencing (WGS) is recommended. The complementary measles virus whole-genome sequencing protocol provides detailed instructions for the sequencing of measles virus detected in wastewater, enabling further genetic characterisation and strain identification.

关于 1900 年至 1901 年雅库特地区的 麻疹 流行病

About the measles epidemic in the Yakut region in 1900–1901
作者： Спиридон Спиридонович Слепцов, Снежана Спиридоновна Слепцова, S. S. Sleptsov, S. S. Sleptsova
摘要中文翻译尽管麻疹 麻疹 暴发在前苏联时期在雅库特被多次记录,但最大的麻疹,在1900-1901年,尚未在科学文献中详细描述,并且其记录的规模尚未从现有来源确定。首次重建 1900 年至 1901 年雅库特最大规模 麻疹 流行病的过程和后果,并利用现有档案来源确定其记录规模。材料与方法。对俄罗斯东北部 70 个东正教教堂和两个传教站的教区登记册进行了比较分析。对萨哈共和国国家档案馆(雅库特)和伊尔库茨克地区国家档案馆保存的 1899 年至 1903 年的记录进行了检查,重点是记录在案的 麻疹 死亡事件。最早有记录的麻疹死亡事件发生在1899年夏天的博代博地区。1900年春天,感染蔓延到奥列克明斯克和维柳伊斯克地区。死亡率 于 1900 年 11 月在雅库茨克地区达到顶峰,主要发生在勒拿河与阿姆加河交汇处。1900 年至 1901 年冬季,维尔霍扬斯克地区的教区登记册中记录了 麻疹 死亡人数,其中包括北极圈以外的布伦教区和乌斯季亚纳教区。从 1901 年初开始,麻疹 沿着维尔霍扬斯克-科雷马路线在科雷马地区广泛传播。研究结果追踪了感染的传播情况,描述了死亡人数的年龄和性别分布,并记录了援助的组织情况。考虑了迁徙和生活条件在疫情中可能发挥的作用。历史资料有助于了解过去的流行病学过程,并为预防传染病的现代策略的讨论提供信息。特别关注 死亡率 峰值、地理覆盖范围以及死亡人数的年龄和性别分布。科累马地区死者中成年人的比例高于西部和中部地区。低群体免疫力仅被认为是这些差异的可能解释。科雷马地区体现了医务人员、政治流亡者和当地居民在组织援助方面发挥的作用。研究表明,即使在人口密度低和交通基础设施有限的地区,麻疹 暴发 也可能发展成为大规模灾难。

About the measles epidemic in the Yakut region in 1900–1901

Authors: Спиридон Спиридонович Слепцов, Снежана Спиридоновна Слепцова, S. S. Sleptsov, S. S. Sleptsova
Original abstract (English)
Although measles outbreaks were repeatedly recorded in Yakutia during the pre-Soviet period, the largest outbreak, in 1900–1901, has not yet been described in detail in the scientific literature, and its documented scale has not been established from the available sources. To reconstruct, for the first time, the course and consequences of the largest measles epidemic in Yakutia in 1900–1901 and establish its documented scale using available archival sources. Materials & Methods. A comparative analysis was conducted of parish registers from 70 Orthodox churches and two mission stations in northeastern Russia. Records for 1899–1903 held by the National Archive of the Republic of Sakha (Yakutia) and the State Archive of the Irkutsk Region were examined, with a focus on documented measles deaths. The earliest recorded measles deaths occurred in the Bodaibo area in the summer of 1899. In the spring of 1900, the infection spread to the Olekminsk and Vilyuisk districts. Mortality peaked in the Yakutsk district in November 1900, predominantly in the Lena–Amga interfluve. In the winter of 1900–1901, measles deaths were recorded in parish registers in the Verkhoyansk district, including the Bulun and Ust-Yana parishes beyond the Arctic Circle. From the beginning of 1901, measles spread widely across the Kolyma district along the Verkhoyansk–Kolyma route. The findings trace the spread of infection, describe the age and sex distribution of deaths and document the organization of assistance. The possible role of migration and living conditions in the epidemic is considered. Historical sources contribute to understanding past epidemiological processes and inform discussion of modern strategies for preventing infectious diseases. Particular attention is paid to mortality peaks, geographical coverage and the age and sex distribution of deaths. The proportion of adults among the deceased was higher in the Kolyma district than in the western and central districts. Low population immunity is considered only as a possible explanation for these differences. The Kolyma district illustrates the role of medical workers, political exiles and local residents in organizing assistance. The study demonstrates that a measles outbreak can develop into a large-scale disaster even in areas with low population density and limited transport infrastructure.

传染病中心工作人员麻疹后麻疹体液免疫动态的纵向研究

Longitudinal study of the dynamics of post-vaccination humoral immunity against measles among staff of an infectious disease centre
作者： В. И. Фомичев, Харит Сусанна Михайловна, Голева Ольга Владимировна, Ольга Витальевна Иозефович, Вячеслав Альбертович Ратников, Самойлова Ирина Геннадьевна, Крылова Галина Никитовна, V. I. Fomichev, S. M. Kharit, O. V. Goleva
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
麻疹 仍然是一个重大的公共卫生问题,医护人员是感染的高危人群,也是院内 传播 的潜在来源。

Longitudinal study of the dynamics of post-vaccination humoral immunity against measles among staff of an infectious disease centre

Authors: В. И. Фомичев, Харит Сусанна Михайловна, Голева Ольга Владимировна, Ольга Витальевна Иозефович, Вячеслав Альбертович Ратников, Самойлова Ирина Геннадьевна, Крылова Галина Никитовна, V. I. Fomichev, S. M. Kharit, O. V. Goleva
Original abstract (English)
Measles remains a significant public health concern, and healthcare workers represent a high-risk group for infection and a potential source of nosocomial transmission. Data on the long-term dynamics of post-vaccination humoral immunity to measles among healthcare workers remain limited. To study the long-term dynamics of anti-measles IgG antibody levels among staff of a medical organization and to identify factors associated with the direction of these changes. Materials & Methods. A longitudinal study was conducted using serological screening data for measles IgG antibodies among staff of a medical organization collected from 2013 to 2025. Data from 571 measurements in 216 individuals with at least two IgG determinations by ELISA («VektoKor’ IgG» test system) were analyzed. A concentration ≥ 0.18 IU/mL was considered protective. The mean IgG concentration was 1.22 IU/mL, with a protective level recorded in 86.5 % of measurements. Ordinary linear regression across all 571 measurements yielded a negative slope (β = −0.058 IU/mL/year; R2 = 0.023). Within-participant dependence was not accounted for; therefore, the nominal p < 0.001 does not establish a statistically significant longitudinal trend. Participants were divided into groups according to the direction of IgG change with increasing (48.6 %), decreasing (41.7 %), and stable (9.7 %) titers; occupation was significantly associated with this distribution (an increase was more common among medical staff – 54.2 % vs 34.4 % among non-medical staff; p = 0.031). A strong negative correlation was found between the baseline IgG level and its subsequent annual change (r = –0.582; p < 0.0001), compatible with regression to the mean and mathematical coupling between baseline and change. Among twice-vaccinated individuals with an initially protective antibody level, IgG fell below 0.18 IU/mL at the last measurement in 2 of 41 participants (4.9 %). Measles IgG levels among staff of the infectious disease centre changed in different directions, with high inter-individual variability. IgG increases were more frequent among medical staff; the role of subclinical exposure to measles virus was not established by this study. Loss of the serological protective level in some twicevaccinated participants warrants further study; clinical protection and the effectiveness of additional vaccination were not assessed.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Measles remains a significant public health concern, and healthcare workers represent a high-risk group for infection and a potential source of nosocomial transmission.
Design and population
Participants were divided into groups according to the direction of IgG change with increasing (48.6 %), decreasing (41.7 %), and stable (9.7 %) titers; occupation was significantly associated with this distribution (an increase was more common among medical staff – 54.2 % vs 34.4 % among non-medical staff; p = 0.031).
Core methods

Main results
Within-participant dependence was not accounted for; therefore, the nominal p < 0.001 does not establish a statistically significant longitudinal trend.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

MODERN APPROACHES TO THE PREVENTION OF MEASLES AMONG CHILDREN AND ADULTS

Authors: Ikramova Nargiza Alisher qizi
Original abstract (English)
Measles is one of the most contagious viral infections and remains a significant threat to public health, particularly among young children and individuals without immunity. Despite the availability of effective vaccines, measles outbreaks continue to occur in different countries due to insufficient vaccination coverage, population migration, and gaps in herd immunity. Modern measles prevention includes routine and supplementary immunization, epidemiological surveillance, early detection of cases, isolation of infected individuals, and public health education. Particular importance is given to increasing vaccination coverage among children and adults, especially those belonging to high-risk groups.

根据一项回顾性研究,儿童 麻疹 后 麻疹 的免疫强度

Intensity of post-vaccination immunity against measles in children according to a retrospective study
作者： D. V. Orlov, S. K. Pylaeva, A. Y. Rtischev, O. B. Kovalev, A. A. Guzhavina, O. V. Shamsheva
摘要中文翻译目的:回顾性评估接种麻疹疫苗的儿童在不同年龄和疫苗接种状态下体液免疫强度。材料和方法:回顾性研究纳入了171名1至18岁在儿童市临床医院部门因接触麻疹而接受抗流行措施的患者。通过酶免疫法评估体液免疫强度。结果:在有疫苗接种记录的儿童中,1至6岁接种一次疫苗的患者中血清阴性比例为20.6%,6岁以上接种两次疫苗的患者中血清阴性比例为18.1%。两组患者比例之间无统计学显著差异(p = 0.69)。结论:接种麻疹疫苗的儿童中血清阴性比例较高,超过俄罗斯联邦消费者权益保护和公共健康监督局推荐的7%阈值,需要进一步研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
根据年龄和 疫苗接种 状况,对患有各种躯体疾病的接种 麻疹 的儿童的体液免疫强度进行回顾性评估。
设计与对象
材料与方法:回顾性研究纳入儿童市第二临床医院科室因与麻疹接触而采取抗疫措施的171名1~18岁患者。
核心方法
通过酶免疫测定法评估体液免疫的强度。
主要结果
患者比例之间没有统计学上的显着差异(p = 0.69)。
解释与创新
疫苗接种人群中血清阴性儿童的比例很高,超过了 Rospotrebnadzor 建议的疫苗接种人群中 7% 的阈值,需要进一步研究。
科研与公卫意义
所提供的证据没有明确说明具体的科学或公共卫生含义。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Intensity of post-vaccination immunity against measles in children according to a retrospective study

Authors: D. V. Orlov, S. K. Pylaeva, A. Y. Rtischev, O. B. Kovalev, A. A. Guzhavina, O. V. Shamsheva
Original abstract (English)
Objective: a retrospective assessment of the intensity of humoral immunity in children vaccinated against measles with various somatic diseases, depending on age and vaccination status. Materials and methods: A retrospective study included 171 patients aged 1 to 18 years who were exposed to anti-epidemic measures for contact with measles at the department of the Children's City Clinical Hospital No. The assessment of the intensity of humoral immunity was carried out by enzyme immunoassay. Results: Among children with documented vaccination, the proportion of seronegative patients among those vaccinated once between the ages of 1 and 6 years was 20.6%, among those vaccinated twice over the age of 6 years — 18.1%. There were no statistically significant differences between the proportions of patients ( p = 0.69). Conclusion: The high proportion of seronegative children among the vaccinated population exceeds the threshold recommended by Rospotrebnadzor of 7% among those vaccinated and requires further research.

Core methods

Clinical and Epidemiological Characteristics of Measles in the Belgorod Region
作者： A. A. Agarkova, A. A. Zherdeva, K. S. Bagdasaryan
摘要中文翻译2023-2025 年别尔哥罗德地区儿童和成人 麻疹 课程流行病学和临床实验室特征的比较分析。材料和方法。对 51 例经实验室确诊的 诊断(IgM 至 麻疹病毒)患者的病历进行回顾性分析。对别尔哥罗德地区(2023-2025 年)麻疹 发病率的分析显示,90.1% 的病例是在未接种疫苗的个体中登记的。输入病例占比为37.3%,家庭内接触(59.4%)是该地区传播的主导模式。82.3% 的患者的疾病特征为典型的中度严重程度。主要症状(鼻炎、咳嗽和荨麻疹 — 100%)的普遍性与其他标志物的年龄相关变异性相结合:结膜炎和 Belsky-Filatov-Koplik 斑在成人中更为常见(71.4% 和 80.9%,而儿童为 46.6% 和 43.3%)。全身炎症的实验室症状(ESR 加速、CRP 升高)在成人组中也很普遍。严重病程和并发症在成人中更为常见(19.0% 和 66.6%,儿童为 3.4% 和 26.6%;p
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在比较 2023 年至 2025 年别尔哥罗德地区儿童和成人 麻疹 的流行病学和临床实验室特征,解决该疾病在不同年龄组之间变异性的知识差距。
设计与对象
对 2023 年至 2025 年间别尔哥罗德地区 51 名经实验室确诊为 麻疹病毒 感染的患者的病历进行了回顾性分析。
核心方法
该研究涉及审查病历,以分析 麻疹 病例的流行病学和临床实验室特征,包括年龄分布、传播 模式和症状 麻疹。
主要结果
分析显示,别尔哥罗德地区90.1%的麻疹病例是在未接种疫苗的个体中登记的,其中37.3%的病例是输入性病例,家庭内接触者占该地区传播病例的59.4%。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了针对未接种疫苗人群的 疫苗接种 计划和公共卫生措施以及基于家庭的 传播 动态在控制 麻疹 暴发 方面的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于回顾性病历,可能会引入回忆偏差,而且证据仅限于别尔哥罗德地区,降低了普遍性。证据强度中等。

Clinical and Epidemiological Characteristics of Measles in the Belgorod Region

Authors: A. A. Agarkova, A. A. Zherdeva, K. S. Bagdasaryan
Original abstract (English)
Comparative analysis of epidemiological and clinical-laboratory features of measles course in children and adults in the Belgorod region for the period 2023—2025. Materials and methods. Retrospective analysis of medical records of 51 patients with a laboratory-confirmed diagnosis (IgM to measles virus). Analysis of measles morbidity in the Belgorod region (2023—2025) revealed that 90.1% of cases were registered among unvaccinated individuals. The share of imported cases was 37.3%, and intra-family contacts (59.4%) were the leading mode of transmission within the region. In 82.3% of patients, the disease was characterized by a typical moderate severity. The universality of the main symptoms (rhinitis, cough, and exanthem — 100%) was combined with age-related variability of additional markers: conjunctivitis and Belsky-Filatov-Koplik spots were more common in adults (71.4% and 80.9% vs. 46.6% and 43.3% in children). Laboratory signs of systemic inflammation (accelerated ESR, increased CRP) also prevailed in the adult group. Severe course and complications were registered more often in adults (19.0% and 66.6% vs. 3.4% and 26.6% in children; p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to compare the epidemiological and clinical-laboratory features of measles in children and adults in the Belgorod region during 2023—2025, addressing the knowledge gap regarding the disease's variability across age groups.
Design and population
A retrospective analysis of medical records was conducted on 51 patients with a laboratory-confirmed diagnosis of measles virus infection in the Belgorod region between 2023 and 2025.
Core methods
The study involved reviewing medical records to analyze epidemiological and clinical-laboratory features of measles cases, including age distribution, transmission modes, and symptom prevalence.
Main results
The analysis revealed that 90.1% of measles cases in the Belgorod region were registered among unvaccinated individuals, with 37.3% of cases being imported and intra-family contacts accounting for 59.4% of transmission within the region.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the significant role of unvaccinated individuals in measles transmission and the dominance of intra-family spread, offering novel insights into the epidemiological patterns of measles in the region.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of vaccination programs and public health measures targeting unvaccinated populations and family-based transmission dynamics in controlling measles outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on retrospective medical records, which may introduce recall bias, and the evidence is limited to the Belgorod region, reducing generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate.

Oncolytic measles virus reprograms the tumor microenvironment in a vascularized mesothelioma-on-chip model
作者： Nikita Rajkumari, Mégane Willems, Judith Fresquet, Elise Douillard, Magali Devic, Frédéric Tangy, Hortense Perdrieau, Delphine Fradin, Jean‐François Fonteneau, Nicolas Boisgerault
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 胸膜间皮瘤 (PM) 是一种罕见的侵袭性癌症,主要由石棉暴露引起。尽管双重免疫检查点抑制(抗 PD-1/抗 CTLA-4)现已被批准作为一线治疗,但临床获益仅限于一小部分患者,因此需要替代策略。溶瘤病毒代表了一种有前途的治疗方法,因为它们选择性地感染和裂解肿瘤细胞并表现出免疫原性。在 PM 中,我们发现 麻疹病毒 (MV) 的溶瘤活性主要依赖于 I 型干扰素 (IFN-I) 途径的改变。然而,迄今为止,尚未开发出体外模型来模拟复杂的肿瘤微环境 (TME),并准确评估 MV 对 PM 的疗效。方法我们使用模仿 TME 的微流控芯片设计了人性化 3D“芯片上血管化间皮瘤”(VMOC) 模型。VMOC 使用原代内皮细胞、肺成纤维细胞和患者来源的 PM 细胞产生。我们通过共聚焦成像、灌注测定和单细胞 RNA 测序来表征我们模型的功能。通过批量转录组学、多重 ELISA 和反卷积策略评估 MV 对 VMOC 的影响,并与 PM 患者的临床数据进行比较。结果我们首先证明了 VMOC 具有适当的内皮屏障完整性、可灌注性和血管对外部刺激的反应性的功能。我们证明了 VMOC 存在显着的异质性,存在不同的间充质、癌症和内皮亚群。有趣的是,将PM细胞添加到VMOC中既减少了来自TME的细胞中的IFN-I途径,也减少了以IFN-I信号传导特征为特征的新型内皮细胞亚群的丰度。通过内皮网络给予 MV 后,我们检测到 PM 细胞感染和死亡,以及 IFN-I 途径的强烈激活和多种炎症介质的产生。有趣的是,MV 在 VMOC 中诱导的蛋白质组和转录组炎症反应与用溶瘤病毒治疗的 PM 患者诱导的相似。结论 我们开发的 VMOC 模型能够对 MV 感染和 TME 重编程进行体外研究,为更深入地了解 TME 在 OV 反应中的作用以及开发提高临床疗效的新策略铺平道路。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在满足体外模型的需求,以评估溶瘤剂 麻疹病毒 (MV) 对胸膜间皮瘤 (PM) 的疗效,这是一种主要由石棉暴露引起的罕见且侵袭性癌症。尽管双重免疫检查点抑制现已被批准作为一线治疗,但临床获益仅限于一小部分患者,因此需要替代策略。
设计与对象
使用微流控芯片设计了人性化 3D“芯片上血管化间皮瘤”(VMOC) 模型来模拟 PM 的肿瘤微环境 (TME)。该模型是使用原代内皮细胞、肺成纤维细胞和患者来源的 PM 细胞生成的。
核心方法
VMOC 模型通过共聚焦成像、灌注测定和单细胞 RNA 测序进行表征。使用批量转录组学、多重 ELISA 和反卷积策略评估 MV 对 VMOC 的影响,并与 PM 患者的临床数据进行比较。
主要结果
VMOC 功能通过适当的内皮屏障完整性、灌注性和血管对外部刺激的反应性得到证实。该模型表现出不同间充质、癌症和内皮亚群存在的显着异质性。通过内皮网络施用 MV 导致 PM 细胞感染和死亡,以及 I 型干扰素 (IFN-I) 途径的强烈激活,并产生多种炎症介质。
解释与创新
研究表明,MV 在 PM 中的溶瘤活性主要依赖于 IFN-I 途径的改变。VMOC模型能够对MV感染和TME重编程进行体外研究,为更深入地了解TME在OV反应中的作用以及开发提高临床疗效的新策略铺平了道路。
科研与公卫意义
VMOC 模型可用于研究 MV 感染和 TME 重编程,这可能导致开发新策略来提高 PM 的临床疗效。该模型提供了一个在更生理相关的环境中评估 MV 和其他溶瘤病毒的平台。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用非随机体外模型以及需要进一步体内验证。由于所提供的证据仅具有抽象性质,因此证据强度中等。

Oncolytic measles virus reprograms the tumor microenvironment in a vascularized mesothelioma-on-chip model

Authors: Nikita Rajkumari, Mégane Willems, Judith Fresquet, Elise Douillard, Magali Devic, Frédéric Tangy, Hortense Perdrieau, Delphine Fradin, Jean‐François Fonteneau, Nicolas Boisgerault
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Pleural mesothelioma (PM) is a rare and aggressive cancer primarily caused by asbestos exposure. Although dual immune checkpoint inhibition (anti-PD-1/anti-CTLA-4) is now approved as first-line therapy, clinical benefit is limited to a small subset of patients, necessitating the need for alternative strategies. Oncolytic viruses represent a promising therapeutic approach as they selectively infect and lyse tumor cells and display immunogenic properties. In PM, we showed that the oncolytic activity of measles virus (MV) is primarily dependent on alterations in the type I interferon (IFN-I) pathway. However, to date no in vitro models have yet been developed to mimic a sophisticated tumor microenvironment (TME), and accurately evaluate MV efficacy in PM. Methods We designed a humanized 3D “vascularized mesothelioma-on-chip” (VMOC) model using microfluidic chips that mimic a TME. VMOCs are generated using primary endothelial cells, lung fibroblasts and patient-derived PM cells. We characterized the functionality of our model by confocal imaging, perfusion assay and single-cell RNA sequencing. The impact of MV on the VMOC was assessed by bulk transcriptomics, multiplex ELISA and deconvolution strategies, and compared to clinical data in patients with PM. Results We first proved VMOC functionality with an appropriate endothelial barrier integrity, perfusability, and vascular responsiveness to external stimuli. We demonstrated significant heterogeneity in the VMOC, with the presence of different mesenchymal, cancer and endothelial subpopulations. Interestingly, the addition of PM cells to the VMOC reduced both the IFN-I pathway in cells from the TME, and the abundance of a novel endothelial cell subset characterized by an IFN-I signaling signature. Following MV administration via the endothelial network, we detected PM cell infection and death, as well as strong activation of the IFN-I pathway with the production of multiple inflammatory mediators. Interestingly, the proteomic and transcriptomic inflammatory response induced by MV in VMOCs was similar to that induced in PM patients treated with an oncolytic virus. Conclusions The VMOC model we developed enables in vitro investigation of MV infection and TME reprogramming, paving the way for a deeper understanding of the TME role in OV responses and the development of novel strategies to improve clinical efficacy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for in vitro models to evaluate the efficacy of oncolytic measles virus (MV) in pleural mesothelioma (PM), a rare and aggressive cancer primarily caused by asbestos exposure. Although dual immune checkpoint inhibition is now approved as first-line therapy, clinical benefit is limited to a small subset of patients, necessitating alternative strategies.
Design and population
A humanized 3D ‘vascularized mesothelioma-on-chip’ (VMOC) model was designed using microfluidic chips to mimic the tumor microenvironment (TME) of PM. The model was generated using primary endothelial cells, lung fibroblasts, and patient-derived PM cells.
Core methods
The VMOC model was characterized by confocal imaging, perfusion assays, and single-cell RNA sequencing. The impact of MV on the VMOC was assessed using bulk transcriptomics, multiplex ELISA, and deconvolution strategies, and compared to clinical data in patients with PM.
Main results
The VMOC functionality was confirmed with appropriate endothelial barrier integrity, perfusability, and vascular responsiveness to external stimuli. The model demonstrated significant heterogeneity with the presence of different mesenchymal, cancer, and endothelial subpopulations. MV administration via the endothelial network led to PM cell infection and death, as well as strong activation of the type I interferon (IFN-I) pathway with the production of multiple inflammatory mediators.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrated that the oncolytic activity of MV in PM is primarily dependent on alterations in the IFN-I pathway. The VMOC model enabled in vitro investigation of MV infection and TME reprogramming, paving the way for a deeper understanding of the TME role in OV responses and the development of novel strategies to improve clinical efficacy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The VMOC model can be used to investigate MV infection and TME reprogramming, which could lead to the development of novel strategies to improve clinical efficacy in PM. This model provides a platform for evaluating MV and other oncolytic viruses in a more physiologically relevant setting.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a non-randomized in vitro model and the need for further validation in vivo. The evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the evidence provided.

废水测序可实现可扩展 麻疹 监测

Wastewater sequencing enables scalable measles surveillance
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译麻疹 的基本再生数(R0):系统评价。对 麻疹病毒 跨 暴发 设置的基本繁殖数进行回顾。基于多重 PCR 的方法对废水中的 麻疹病毒 进行全 基因组 测序。这项预印本研究介绍了 Freyja 2,这是我们更新的生物信息学工具,可分析废水和复杂的环境样本,以追踪病原体 进化 和传播。南非 麻疹 2024 年至 2025 年 暴发 期间的综合临床和废水监测实现了病毒传播和 进化 的高分辨率跟踪。废水样本的全 基因组 扩增子测序揭示了基因型 患病率 的变化、详细的 传播 链接以及之前未报告的省际传播。这是订阅内容的预览,可通过您的机构访问 访问选项 访问 Nature 和 54 种其他 Nature Portfolio 期刊 获取 Nature+,我们最有价值的在线访问订阅 订阅该期刊 接收 12 期印刷刊和在线访问 购买本文 - 在 SpringerLink 上购买 - 即时访问全文 PDF。39.95 美元 价格可能需缴纳地方税,该税费在结账时计算。参考资料 - Guerra,F. 17,e420–e428 (2017)。评估 麻疹 废水 监测 可行性时的时间、空间和方法考虑因素。本文利用病毒脱落测量和建模研究了 麻疹 废水 监测 的可行性。Dynabeads 废水病毒富集和总核酸提取。Protocols.io https://doi.org/10.17504/protocols.io.n2bvj9j5nlk5/v1 (2025)。使用磁珠技术进行病毒富集和核酸提取的详细方案。Protocols.io https://doi.org/10.17504/protocols.io.n92ld41wol5b/v2 (2026)....
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查在南非 麻疹 期间使用废水测序进行可扩展的 麻疹 监测 的可行性,以满足对病毒传播和 进化 进行高分辨率跟踪的需求。
设计与对象
该研究对2024年至2025年麻疹 暴发期间来自南非的废水样本进行了全基因组扩增子测序,涉及多个省份,重点关注基因型患病率和传播链接。
核心方法
使用 Dynabeads 技术对废水样品进行富集并提取总核酸,然后对 麻疹病毒 进行基于多重 PCR 的全 基因组 测序。
主要结果
废水样本的全 基因组 测序揭示了基因型 患病率 的变化、详细的 传播 链接以及之前未报告的 麻疹病毒 的省际传播。
解释与创新
该研究将这些发现解释为能够对 麻疹病毒 传播和 进化 进行高分辨率跟踪,这在使用废水测序实现可扩展的 监测 方面是新颖的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为 麻疹 的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略提供信息,从而增强对 暴发 的公共卫生应对。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认设计上的局限性,例如需要进一步验证和推广到其他环境,并将证据强度评估为中等。

Wastewater sequencing enables scalable measles surveillance

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
The basic reproduction number (R0) of measles: a systematic review. A review on the basic reproductive number of measles virus across outbreak settings. Multiplex PCR-based method for whole-genome sequencing of measles virus from wastewater. This preprint study introduces Freyja 2, our updated bioinformatics tool that analyzes wastewater and complex environmental samples to track pathogen evolution and spread. Integrated clinical and wastewater monitoring during the 2024–2025 outbreaks of measles in South Africa enabled high-resolution tracking of virus spread and evolution. Whole-genome amplicon sequencing of wastewater samples revealed shifts in genotype prevalence, detailed transmission links and previously unreported interprovincial spread. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the feasibility of using wastewater sequencing for scalable measles surveillance during outbreaks in South Africa, addressing the need for high-resolution tracking of virus spread and evolution.
Design and population
The study employed whole-genome amplicon sequencing of wastewater samples from South Africa during the 2024–2025 measles outbreaks, involving multiple provinces and focusing on genotype prevalence and transmission links.
Core methods
Wastewater samples were enriched and total nucleic acid extracted using Dynabeads technology, followed by multiplex PCR-based whole-genome sequencing of measles virus.
Main results
Whole-genome sequencing of wastewater samples revealed shifts in genotype prevalence, detailed transmission links, and previously unreported interprovincial spread of measles virus.
Interpretation and novelty
The study interpreted these findings as enabling high-resolution tracking of measles virus spread and evolution, which is novel in using wastewater sequencing for scalable surveillance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for measles, enhancing public health responses to outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges limitations in design, such as the need for further validation and generalizability to other settings, and assesses the evidence strength as moderate.

表征麻疹持续存在的元种群动态相对强度

Characterizing the relative strength of metapopulation dynamics in measles persistence
作者： Bhatia, D., McKee, A., Ferrari, M.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
背景/目标 麻疹 是一种高度传染性、疫苗 可预防的疾病,尽管持续开展大规模控制工作,但在世界大部分地区仍然持续存在。
设计与对象
方法/结果我们表明,随着 疫苗接种 减少局部 麻疹 患病率,集合种群定殖和灭绝事件主导区域持久性。
核心方法
我们使用以下方法证明了这一点:1)季节性强制、随机集合种群分区模型;2) 美国的历史麻疹 发病率;3) 尼日利亚最近的地方层面 麻疹 发病率。
主要结果
In Nigeria, roundness is well correlated with observed vaccine coverage, with a Pearson's r of 0.70 (95% CI: 0.57, 0.80), discriminating between northern states dominated by local persistence of measles and southern states where persistence is maintained by regional dynamics.
解释与创新
结论 确定 麻疹 的持续存在方式对于控制 麻疹 非常重要;如果持久性主要是本地的,或者如果持久性是由周围区域的连接驱动的,则 疫苗接种 策略可能会有所不同,即使年度 发病率 具有可比性。
科研与公卫意义
我们创建了一种新颖的无标度测量方法来量化集合种群动态对持久性和圆度的贡献,定义为按比例缩放的每月最大和最小 发病率 的几何平均值。
局限与证据强度
我们的方法可以在其他措施(连通性、移动性、实验室确诊疾病)尚未广泛应用的领域发挥重要作用。

Characterizing the relative strength of metapopulation dynamics in measles persistence

Authors: Bhatia, D., McKee, A., Ferrari, M.
Original abstract (English)
Background/Aims Measles is a highly infectious, vaccine-preventable disease that remains persistent across much of the world despite continued large-scale control efforts. Local dynamics of measles are shaped by births of susceptible individuals and seasonal drivers of transmission, while broader regional dynamics are shaped by population mobility and metapopulation connectivity. Incidence data, useful for tracking the progress of measles control, are frequently reported only at the aggregate level (e.g., states/provinces), and persistence at this scale may arise because of the independent persistence in all local sub-units or because of regional metapopulation dynamics. Disentangling the contribution of local versus regional persistence is critical in identifying effective strategies for control. Methods/Results We show, as vaccination reduces local measles prevalence, metapopulation colonization and extinction events dominate regional persistence. This manifests as a change in the seasonal distribution of cases throughout the year; incidence becomes more uniformly distributed through the year even with no change in underlying seasonal drivers. We demonstrate this using: 1) a seasonally forced, stochastic metapopulation compartmental model; 2) historical measles incidence in the USA; and 3) recent subnational-level measles incidence in Nigeria. We create a novel, scale-free measure to quantify the contribution of metapopulation dynamics to persistence, roundness, defined as the geometric mean of the scaled maximum and minimum monthly incidence. A low value of roundness indicates strong local persistence dominated by the seasonal attractor, while a high value indicates strong metapopulation persistence. We illustrate the properties of this measure using simulations and observed data. In the USA, roundness nearly doubled after the introduction of the measles-containing vaccine. In Nigeria, roundness is well correlated with observed vaccine coverage, with a Pearson's r of 0.70 (95% CI: 0.57, 0.80), discriminating between northern states dominated by local persistence of measles and southern states where persistence is maintained by regional dynamics. Conclusion Identifying how measles persists is important in its control; vaccination strategies may differ if persistence is largely local, or if persistence is driven by connectivity from surrounding areas, even where yearly incidence is comparable. Our approach can be valuable areas where other measures (connectivity, mobility, laboratory confirmed illness) are not widely available.

Conclusion Identifying how measles persists is important in its control; vaccination strategies may differ if persistence is largely local, or if persistence is driven by connectivity from surrounding areas, even where yearly incidence is comparable.
Scientific and public-health significance
We create a novel, scale-free measure to quantify the contribution of metapopulation dynamics to persistence, roundness, defined as the geometric mean of the scaled maximum and minimum monthly incidence.
Limitations and evidence strength
Our approach can be valuable areas where other measures (connectivity, mobility, laboratory confirmed illness) are not widely available.
