患病率 埃塞俄比亚中部东古拉格地区 Kebeles 农村五岁以下儿童腹泻及其决定因素:一项横断面研究。

Prevalence of diarrhea and its determinants among children under five years of age in rural Kebeles of East Gurage Zone, central Ethiopia: A cross-sectional study.
作者： Melaku Tesfaye, Seid Tiku Mereta, Dessalegn Dadi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
腹泻病仍然是五岁以下儿童死亡的主要原因,特别是在资源有限的环境中。尽管提供了有效的水、环境卫生和个人卫生 (WASH) 干预措施,但埃塞俄比亚的腹泻负担仍然很高。
设计与对象
这项基线调查是四组整群随机试验 (cRCT) 的一部分。使用 Hayes 和 Bennett 公式,我们将样本量设置为 840 名参与者(每组 210 名),置信度为 95%,功效为 80%。
核心方法
数据在 Epi Data 3.1 中进行清理,并在 SPSS 25 中进行分析。使用双变量和多变量广义估计方程 (GEE) 模型检查儿童腹泻相关因素,考虑聚类,显着性设置为 p < 0.05 和 95% CI。
主要结果
研究区域的腹泻 患病率 较高 (25.7%) (95% CI: 22.8-28.7)。可靠性测试显示内部一致性较弱,知识的克伦巴赫阿尔法为0.36,实践的克朗巴赫阿尔法为0.46,财富的克朗巴赫阿尔法为0.40,反映的项目较少且领域不同。
解释与创新
Maternal age 24-34 (AOR = 0.38, 95% CI: 0.27-0.53), hand washing at critical times (AOR = 0.20, 95% CI: 0.07-0.58), both soap and water use (AOR = 0.09, 95% CI: 0.03-0.33), using tightly covered and clean water containers (AOR = 0.17, 95% CI: 0.03-0.91), measles vaccination (AOR = 0.07, 95% CI: 0.04-0.13), fetching water <30 minutes round-trip (AOR = 0.26, 95% CI: 0.12-0.57), and latrine availability (AOR = 0.28, 95% CI: 0.19-0.43) were significantly associated with lower odds of under-five diarrhea.
科研与公卫意义
四分之一的儿童患有腹泻(25.7%;95% CI:22.8-28.7),这表明研究地区,特别是农村社区,承受着巨大的负担。加强 WASH 干预措施并促进政府、地方当局和发展伙伴之间的协调努力对于减轻这一负担至关重要。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据未报告与 麻疹病毒 相关的具体限制。

Prevalence of diarrhea and its determinants among children under five years of age in rural Kebeles of East Gurage Zone, central Ethiopia: A cross-sectional study.

Authors: Melaku Tesfaye, Seid Tiku Mereta, Dessalegn Dadi
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Diarrheal disease remains a major cause of death among children under five, particularly in resource-limited settings. Despite the availability of effective water, sanitation, and hygiene (WASH) interventions, the burden of diarrhea remains high in Ethiopia. This study aims to assess the prevalence of diarrhea and its associated factors among under-five children in East Gurage Zone, central Ethiopia. METHODOLOGY: This baseline survey was part of a four-arm cluster randomized trial (cRCT). Using the Hayes and Bennett formula, we set the sample size at 840 participants (210 per arm) with 95% confidence and 80% power. Data were cleaned in Epi Data 3.1 and analyzed in SPSS 25. Reliability testing showed weak internal consistency, with Cronbach's alpha of 0.36 for knowledge, 0.46 for practice, and 0.40 for wealth, reflecting few items and distinct domains. Childhood diarrhea associated factors were examined using bivariate and multivariate Generalized Estimating Equations (GEE) models, accounting for clustering, with significance set at p < 0.05 and 95% CI. RESULTS: Diarrhea prevalence was high in the study area (25.7%) (95% CI: 22.8-28.7). Maternal age 24-34 (AOR = 0.38, 95% CI: 0.27-0.53), hand washing at critical times (AOR = 0.20, 95% CI: 0.07-0.58), both soap and water use (AOR = 0.09, 95% CI: 0.03-0.33), using tightly covered (AOR = 0.17, 95% CI: 0.03-0.91) and clean water containers (AOR = 0.47, 95% CI: 0.26-0.84), measles vaccination (AOR = 0.07, 95% CI: 0.04-0.13), fetching water <30 minutes round-trip (AOR = 0.26, 95% CI: 0.12-0.57), and latrine availability (AOR = 0.28, 95% CI: 0.19-0.43) were significantly associated with lower odds of under-five diarrhea. CONCLUSION: Diarrhea affected one in four children (25.7%; 95% CI: 22.8-28.7), indicating a substantial burden in the study area, particularly in rural communities. Strengthening WASH interventions and fostering coordinated efforts among government, local authorities, and development partners is essential to reduce this burden. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov NCT07158632.

保加利亚 50 年期间(1974-2023 年)传染病的 发病率

Incidence of Infectious Diseases in Bulgaria over a 50-Year Period (1974–2023)
作者： Galya Ivanova Gancheva, Ivaylo Pakov
摘要中文翻译目的:调查保加利亚半个世纪重大社会经济转型、医疗改革和地缘政治一体化期间急性传染病发病率的长期趋势和动态。方法:进行为期50年(1974年至2023年)的回顾性流行病学研究。连续 10 个五年期 队列研究 的汇总数据取自国家统计研究所 (NSI)、国家公共卫生和分析中心 (NCPHA) 以及国家传染病和寄生虫病中心 (NCIPD) 管理的官方法定报告网络。结果:传染病动态的巨大变化与国家免疫战略、经济转型和医疗改革的生效直接相关。大规模免疫预防成功地消除了核心呼吸道和神经侵袭性病原体(包括白喉和风疹)或达到历史最低水平。然而,最近的 2020-2023 年指标显示,麻疹-流行性腮腺炎风疹 (MMR) 和百日咳覆盖率并非最佳,引发了百日咳发病率和婴儿 死亡率 的显着上升趋势。缺乏大规模 疫苗接种 框架的病原体,如水痘和猩红热,在遏制措施放松后表现出大流行后的急剧复苏。气候驱动的变化正在扩大 媒介 传播的足迹,例如西尼罗河热。此外,COVID-19 大流行带来了大量的诊断报告假象和沉重的超额 死亡率 负担。结论:长期 疫苗 数据证实,疫苗 可预防疾病总体持续下降,同时肠道、新出现的 媒介 传播疾病和非 疫苗 可预防呼吸道感染的相对比例有所扩大。跟踪这些动态为减轻 疫苗 犹豫和改变生态疾病边界提供了重要的公共卫生基线。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
调查保加利亚半个世纪以来重大社会经济转型、医疗改革和地缘政治一体化期间急性传染病发病率的长期趋势和动态。
设计与对象
进行了一项涵盖 50 年(1974 年至 2023 年)的回顾性流行病学研究。
核心方法
连续 10 个五年期 队列研究 的汇总数据取自国家统计研究所 (NSI)、国家公共卫生和分析中心 (NCPHA) 以及国家传染病和寄生虫病中心 (NCIPD) 管理的官方法定报告网络。
主要结果
传染病动态的急剧变化与国家免疫战略、经济转型和医疗改革的生效直接相关。大规模免疫预防成功地消除了核心呼吸道和神经侵袭性病原体(包括白喉和风疹)或达到历史最低水平。然而,最近的 2020-2023 年指标显示,麻疹-流行性腮腺炎风疹 (MMR) 和百日咳覆盖率并非最佳,引发了百日咳发病率和婴儿 死亡率 的显着上升趋势。气候驱动的变化正在扩大 媒介 传播的足迹,例如西尼罗河热。此外,COVID-19 大流行带来了大量的诊断报告假象和沉重的超额 死亡率 负担。
解释与创新
作者解释了这些发现,指出大规模免疫预防在实现核心呼吸道和神经侵袭性病原体消除模式方面取得的成功,但也强调了最近对 MMR 和百日咳的次优覆盖,这导致了百日咳发病率和婴儿 死亡率 的上升趋势。他们还注意到 媒介 传播的足迹不断扩大,以及 COVID-19 大流行对诊断报告和 死亡率 的影响。
科研与公卫意义
长期 监测 数据证实,疫苗 可预防疾病的总体持续下降,同时肠道、新出现的 媒介 传播的疾病和非 疫苗 可预防的呼吸道感染的相对比例不断扩大。跟踪这些动态为减轻 疫苗 犹豫和改变生态疾病边界提供了重要的公共卫生基线。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到数据的回顾性以及 COVID-19 大流行带来的诊断假象的可能性的限制。证据强度中等。

Incidence of Infectious Diseases in Bulgaria over a 50-Year Period (1974–2023)

Authors: Galya Ivanova Gancheva, Ivaylo Pakov
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To investigate the long-term trends and dynamics of acute infectious diseases morbidity in Bulgaria across half of century of significant socio-economic transitions, healthcare reforms, and geopolitical integrations. Methods: retrospective epidemiological study was conducted covering a 50-year period (1974–2023). Aggregated data from 10 consecutive five-year cohorts were extracted from official statutory reporting networks managed by the National Statistical Institute (NSI), the National Center for Public Health and Analyses (NCPHA), and the National Center for Infectious and Parasitic Diseases (NCIPD). Results: Drastic shifts in infectious disease dynamics correlate directly with national immunization strategies, economic transitions, and the entry into force of healthcare reforms. Mass immunoprophylaxis achieved successful elimination patterns or historic minimums for core respiratory and neuro-invasive pathogens, including diphtheria and rubella. However, recent 2020-2023 metrics reveal sub-optimal measles-mumpsrubella (MMR) and pertussis coverage, sparking a notable upward trend in pertussis morbidity and infant mortality. Pathogens lacking mass vaccination frameworks, such as varicella and scarlet fever, exhibited sharp post-pandemic resurgences following the relaxation of containment measures. Climate-driven shifts are expanding vector-borne footprints, such as West Nile fever. Furthermore, the COVID-19 pandemic introduced substantial diagnostic reporting artefacts and a heavy excess mortality burden. Conclusions: Long-term surveillance data confirms a persistent overall decline in vaccine-preventable diseases alongside expanding relative shares of intestinal, emerging vector-borne, and non-vaccine-preventable respiratory infections. Tracking these dynamics provides a vital public health baseline for mitigating vaccine hesitancy and changing ecological disease boundaries.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To investigate the long-term trends and dynamics of acute infectious diseases morbidity in Bulgaria across half of a century of significant socio-economic transitions, healthcare reforms, and geopolitical integrations.
Design and population
A retrospective epidemiological study was conducted covering a 50-year period (1974–2023).
Core methods
Aggregated data from 10 consecutive five-year cohorts were extracted from official statutory reporting networks managed by the National Statistical Institute (NSI), the National Center for Public Health and Analyses (NCPHA), and the National Center for Infectious and Parasitic Diseases (NCIPD).
Main results
Drastic shifts in infectious disease dynamics correlate directly with national immunization strategies, economic transitions, and the entry into force of healthcare reforms. Mass immunoprophylaxis achieved successful elimination patterns or historic minimums for core respiratory and neuro-invasive pathogens, including diphtheria and rubella. However, recent 2020-2023 metrics reveal sub-optimal measles-mumpsrubella (MMR) and pertussis coverage, sparking a notable upward trend in pertussis morbidity and infant mortality. Climate-driven shifts are expanding vector-borne footprints, such as West Nile fever. Furthermore, the COVID-19 pandemic introduced substantial diagnostic reporting artefacts and a heavy excess mortality burden.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings, noting the success of mass immunoprophylaxis in achieving elimination patterns for core respiratory and neuro-invasive pathogens, but also highlighting the recent sub-optimal coverage for MMR and pertussis, which has led to an upward trend in pertussis morbidity and infant mortality. They also note the expanding vector-borne footprints and the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic on diagnostic reporting and mortality.
Scientific and public-health significance
Long-term surveillance data confirms a persistent overall decline in vaccine-preventable diseases alongside expanding relative shares of intestinal, emerging vector-borne, and non-vaccine-preventable respiratory infections. Tracking these dynamics provides a vital public health baseline for mitigating vaccine hesitancy and changing ecological disease boundaries.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the retrospective nature of the data and the potential for diagnostic artefacts introduced by the COVID-19 pandemic. The evidence strength is moderate.

古老的 p53–miR-128 轴通过抑制 Nectin-4 来限制粘膜和胎盘病毒进入

An Ancient p53–miR-128 Axis Restricts Mucosal and Placental Viral Entry by Suppressing Nectin-4
作者： Boominathan L
摘要中文翻译宿主 细胞表面受体决定病毒趋向性、组织进入和跨屏障 传播。尽管 Nectin-4(由 PVRL4 编码)最近已被确定为主要呼吸道和致畸病原体(包括 麻疹病毒 和风疹病毒)的重要上皮受体,但控制其表达的内源性 麻疹病毒 机制仍不清楚。由于本研究中没有物理湿实验室实验,因此我们利用先进的计算系统-生物学建模、网络集成和预测性多组学模拟。在这里,我们发现了一条进化上保守的 宿主 限制途径,其中古老的 抗病毒药物 守护者 p53 与长寿因子 FOXO3a 协调部署 microRNA-128 (miR-128),从而在转录后沉默 PVRL4。对公共表达库 (GEO/Harmonizome) 的系统计算机评估揭示了 p53 通路激活状态与极化气道上皮的 PVRL4 表面密度之间存在强大的负相关性。通过模拟表面 Nectin-4 密度的抑制,该预测轴阻断了 Nectin-4-tropic 病毒所需的受体介导的膜融合。这些计算结果将 p53–FOXO3a–miR-128–Nectin-4 轴定位为重要的粘膜防御机制,展示了古老的 宿主 限制网络如何针对专门的病毒进入门户。

An Ancient p53–miR-128 Axis Restricts Mucosal and Placental Viral Entry by Suppressing Nectin-4

Authors: Boominathan L
Original abstract (English)
Host cell-surface receptors dictate viral tropism, tissue entry, and cross-barrier transmission. Although Nectin-4 (encoded by PVRL4) has recently been established as the essential epithelial receptor for major respiratory and teratogenic pathogens—including Measles virus and Rubella virus—the endogenous host mechanisms governing its expression remain poorly defined. Because physical wet-lab experimentation is absent in this study, we utilize advanced computational systems-biology modeling, network integration, and predictive multi-omics simulations. Here, we uncover an evolutionarily conserved host restriction pathway wherein the ancient antiviral guardian p53 coordinates with the longevity factor FOXO3a to deploy microRNA-128 (miR-128), which post-transcriptionally silences PVRL4. Systematic in silico evaluation of public expression repositories (GEO/Harmonizome) reveals a robust inverse correlation between p53 pathway activation states and PVRL4 surface density across polarized airway epithelia. By modeling the repression of surface Nectin-4 density, this predictive axis blocks the receptor-mediated membrane fusion required by Nectin-4-tropic viruses. These computational findings position the p53–FOXO3a–miR-128–Nectin-4 axis as a vital mucosal defense mechanism, demonstrating how ancient host restriction networks target specialized viral entry portals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Host cell-surface receptors dictate viral tropism, tissue entry, and cross-barrier transmission.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
Because physical wet-lab experimentation is absent in this study, we utilize advanced computational systems-biology modeling, network integration, and predictive multi-omics simulations.
Main results
Systematic in silico evaluation of public expression repositories (GEO/Harmonizome) reveals a robust inverse correlation between p53 pathway activation states and PVRL4 surface density across polarized airway epithelia.
Interpretation and novelty
By modeling the repression of surface Nectin-4 density, this predictive axis blocks the receptor-mediated membrane fusion required by Nectin-4-tropic viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

解决 疫苗 在肺部疾病培训中的犹豫。

Addressing vaccine hesitancy in pulmonary disease training.
作者： Mazer AJ, Meisenberg BR, Adrien J Mazer, Barry R Meisenberg, Meisenberg BR., Adrien J. Mazer, Barry R. Meisenberg
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
具体的 疫苗 神话包括“自然”免疫力的优越性、免疫系统过载/减弱的概念、疫苗 无效、错误或高估不良副作用(即自闭症谱系障碍)的风险,以及定制 疫苗 时间表以适应“太多”疫苗。
设计与对象
According to estimates from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC), between 1994 and 2023 childhood vaccines prevented roughly 508 million lifetime illnesses, 32 million hospitalizations, and 1,129,000 deaths, and netted $540 billion savings in direct costs and $2.7 trillion in societal costs.
核心方法
传达 疫苗 建议:使用假定格式向患者提供强有力的 疫苗 建议(即,医疗保健提供者不会问“您今天对肺炎球菌 疫苗 感觉如何?”,而是说“您今天将收到肺炎球菌 疫苗。”) 疫苗-犹豫的患者,例如提出开放式问题、提供自主权的肯定和支持、反思之前的患者经历以及共享信息。
主要结果
最初,儿科住院医师免疫教育缺乏一致性,正如 92 名儿科项目总监协会成员在 2014 年完成的一项调查所指出的那样,该调查显示 59% 的项目缺乏关于 疫苗 安全的正式培训来解决家长的担忧。
解释与创新
此外,美国儿科委员会普通儿科认证考试由 24 个领域组成,其中第一个领域是预防儿科/儿童保健,占考试的 12%。

巴基斯坦卡拉奇高风险城市住区的气候变化和常规儿童 疫苗 吸收:纵向固定效应面板分析

Climatic variability and routine childhood vaccine uptake in high-risk urban settlements of Karachi, Pakistan: a longitudinal fixed-effects panel analysis
作者： Saqib Ur Rehman, Ammarah Ali, Wardah Ahmed, Zahid Memon, Zafar Fatmi, Rehman Su
摘要中文翻译气候变化可能会扰乱快速发展的城市的常规免疫接种,这些城市的非正规住区将高气候暴露与脆弱的卫生服务结合在一起,但来自低收入和中等收入国家的纵向城市证据有限。我们使用 266 个设施月观察数据(2023 年 6 月至 2024 年 12 月),研究了为巴基斯坦卡拉奇的 9 个高风险城市工会理事会提供服务的 14 个公共和私人卫生机构中每月气候条件与常规儿童 疫苗 剂量之间的关联。卡介苗 (BCG)、五价疫苗(Penta-1 至 Penta-3)、灭活脊髓灰质炎病毒 (IPV-1) 和 麻疹-风疹 (MR-1) 疫苗 的每月剂量与基于再分析的最高温度、相对湿度和风速、基于卫星的降雨量、夜间光照 (NTL) 和流域人口相关。为每个 疫苗 拟合具有设施聚类标准误差的单向固定效应面板模型,并进行五次敏感性分析。在主要模型中,风速与所有六种 疫苗 的剂量呈负相关(标准化 β = -0.150 至 -0.491),最高温度与五种剂量呈负相关,相对湿度与所有六种剂量呈正相关。在每个规格中,降雨量与 BCG 剂量呈负相关(β = -0.285,p < 0.001),而在添加滞后的气候条件后,降雨量与 Penta-2 和 MR-1 的正相关并不持续存在。当排除启动月份时,仅 BCG 和 Penta-2 的温度关联持续存在。NTL 与多次就诊剂量 Penta-2、Penta-3 和 IPV-1 相关。因此,疫苗 与城市非正式环境中常规免疫的气候关联有所不同,支持针对 疫苗 的、气候知情的城市免疫规划。

Climatic variability and routine childhood vaccine uptake in high-risk urban settlements of Karachi, Pakistan: a longitudinal fixed-effects panel analysis

Authors: Saqib Ur Rehman, Ammarah Ali, Wardah Ahmed, Zahid Memon, Zafar Fatmi, Rehman Su
Original abstract (English)
Climate variability may disrupt routine immunization in rapidly growing cities, where informal settlements combine high climatic exposure with fragile health services, yet longitudinal urban evidence from low- and middle-income countries is limited. We examined associations between monthly climatic conditions and routine childhood vaccine doses in 14 public and private health facilities serving nine high-risk urban union councils of Karachi, Pakistan, using 266 facility-month observations (June 2023–December 2024). Monthly doses of Bacille Calmette–Guérin (BCG), pentavalent (Penta-1 to Penta-3), inactivated poliovirus (IPV-1) and measles–rubella (MR-1) vaccines were linked with reanalysis-based maximum temperature, relative humidity and wind speed, satellite-based rainfall, night-time light (NTL) and catchment population. One-way fixed-effects panel models with facility-clustered standard errors were fitted for each vaccine, with five sensitivity analyses. In the main models, wind speed was negatively associated with doses of all six vaccines (standardized β = −0.150 to −0.491), maximum temperature with five, and relative humidity positively with all six. Rainfall was negatively associated with BCG doses (β = −0.285, p < 0.001) in every specification, whereas its positive associations with Penta-2 and MR-1 did not persist after lagged climatic terms were added. The temperature association persisted only for BCG and Penta-2 when the start-up months were excluded. NTL was associated with the multi-visit doses Penta-2, Penta-3 and IPV-1. Climatic associations with routine immunization in urban informal settings therefore differ by vaccine, supporting vaccine-specific, climate-informed urban immunization planning.
View research seven-element analysis

Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

A decade of infectious disease trends in Northern Cyprus, 2014–2024

Authors: Syed Sikandar Shah, Aftab Alam, Hadeel Shalabi, Servet Gökşin, Junaid Asghar
Original abstract (English)
Background Longitudinal infectious-disease surveillance is difficult to interpret when case definitions, diagnostic practices, and reporting structures change over time. Northern Cyprus remains underrepresented in multi-disease surveillance literature. We therefore reconstructed and harmonized official notifiable-disease data to characterize major temporal patterns while preserving epidemiologic comparability. Methods A retrospective ecological time-series analysis was conducted using official Ministry of Health surveillance records for major notifiable infections from 2014 to 2025. The primary analytical period was 2014–2024, with 2025 retained descriptively. Disease indicators were harmonized using predefined clinical and epidemiologic rules, while non-equivalent categories were analyzed within separate reporting eras. Official Statistical Institute data were used for independent cross-validation of selected annual totals and for population denominators. Notification rates per 100,000 were calculated for 2019–2024, and formal modeling was restricted to sufficiently stable series. Results Disease patterns were heterogeneous. Several infections remained sparse, whereas measles showed episodic activity with renewed notifications after 2022. Varicella fluctuated substantially, with no significant monotonic trend after adjustment for overdispersion (NRR 0.844, 95% CI 0.664–1.074; p = 0.169). Tuberculosis showed the strongest recent signal, rising from 0.44 per 100,000 in 2022 to 13.42 in 2023 and remaining elevated at 9.32 in 2024. Viral hepatitis and HIV/STI patterns were strongly influenced by surveillance-definition changes. Conclusion Notifiable infections in Northern Cyprus showed distinct temporal patterns, including episodic vaccine-preventable disease activity, continued notification of blood-borne and sexually transmitted infections, and a marked recent tuberculosis signal. To our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive harmonized multi-disease analysis of official surveillance in Northern Cyprus, providing an important epidemiologic baseline for an underrepresented Eastern Mediterranean setting. Stable case definitions, compatible denominators, and stronger laboratory linkage are essential for distinguishing true epidemiologic change from reporting effects and supporting targeted public-health action.

The European Vaccines Hub

Authors: Rino Rappuoli, Isabelle Bekeredjian‐Ding, Pierre Van Damme, James P. Di Santo, Donata Medaglini, Michel Pletschette, Rappuoli R, Bekeredjian-Ding I, Van Damme P, Di Santo JP
Original abstract (English)
One year after the EVH was established, each of the four pillars has initiated a variety of projects and delivered important results. Pillar 3 has designed and fully enrolled a clinical trial to study the vaccine response of the European population to primary and booster immunization against the avian influenza H5N1, and is currently assessing its outcome. The study has the additional scope to establish a study cohort whose peripheral blood cells can be used to discover broadly neutralizing antibodies that recognize epitopes shared between the 2006 and 2026 H5N1 strains. nature immunology Volume 27 | December 2026 | 2363–2365 | 2363 The European Vaccines Hub Rino Rappuoli, Isabelle Bekeredjian-Ding, Pierre Van Damme, James P. Di Santo, Donata Medaglini & Michel Pletschette The European Vaccines Hub is a novel design consortium that assembles leading European organizations involved in vaccine development. The mission is to develop vaccines and monoclonal antibodies ensuring Europe’s pandemics and emerging infections readiness. When the COVID-19 pandemic struck the European Union, it was insufficiently prepared to face the devastating health and economic emergency. Although European biotech contributed significantly to the development of vaccines with products such as Comirnaty, the mRNA vaccine developed by the German BioNTech was eventually licensed to and commercialized by an American company, meaning that very few European medical countermeasures reached the market at the peak of the pandemic. A further weakness identified was that Europe did not partici- pate in any of the pivotal efficacy trials leading to licensure. To address this situation, the European Commission established the Health Emergency Preparedness and Response Authority (HERA) as one of its Directora...

Question and background
To achieve this, the EVH was expected to be organ- ized in an agile manner, allowing it to make quick decisions and to include and engage with any group from industry, biotech or academia that could contribute to development of vaccines and monoclonal antibodies against pathogens with pandemic risk.
Design and population
Pillar 3 has designed and fully enrolled a clinical trial to study the vaccine response of the European population to primary and booster immunization against the avian influenza H5N1, and is currently assessing its outcome.
Core methods
The study has the additional scope to establish a study cohort whose peripheral blood cells can be used to discover broadly neutralizing antibodies that recognize epitopes shared between the 2006 and 2026 H5N1 strains.
Main results
In the aftermath of the COVID-19 pandemic, HERA published a call for proposals for the creation of a European Hub for vaccine development, with the mission of developing, assessing and manufacturing vaccines for priority pathogens with epidemic and pandemic potential to ensure that Europe would be more resilient through increased preparedness.
Interpretation and novelty
The first was the Andes hanta- virus outbreak, reported on 2 May, involving a cruise ship coming out of Argentina1, and the second was the Ebola Bundibugyo outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda that was declared a Public Health Emergency of International Concern on 17 May2.
Scientific and public-health significance
Di Santo 1,6, Donata Medaglini1,7 & Michel Pletschette1,8 1EU Vaccines Hub for Pandemic Preparedness (EVH) on behalf of the EVH consortium https://www.europeanvaccineshub.eu/.
Limitations and evidence strength
Looking to the future In conclusion, the EVH is an organization rooting pandemic prepar- edness within an innovative cross-sectorial ecosystem, which in its first year of existence has shown the potential to deliver innovative countermeasures.

疫苗学2.0

Vaccinology 2.0
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Hogan 和 Norbert Pardi 讨论了其他 RNA 技术的进展,这些技术旨在提高功效、降低反应原性并增强 T 细胞和粘膜 免疫应答。
设计与对象
在 mRNA 疫苗 平台等新技术的推动下,以及在病毒学、疫苗学和结构生物学方面丰富的现有知识的推动下,针对 SARS-CoV-2 的 疫苗 以创纪录的速度开发出来。

主要结果
这些相互作用创造了抵抗力增强的器官嵌入状态,为多种病原体提供了广泛的保护,这种现象被称为“综合器官免疫”。
解释与创新

Pulendran 提出,未来的疫苗学应该从主要关注抗原和免疫相关物转向识别、表征和故意设计这些保护性免疫状态,从而有可能使 疫苗 针对传染病和其他免疫介导的疾病具有更持久和更广泛的保护性。

Vaccinology 2.0

Original abstract (English)
nature immunology Volume 27 | December 2026 | 2355 | 2355 Vaccinology 2.0 The COVID-19 pandemic highlighted the success of vaccine development, but more improvements in vaccine design, equity, manufacturing and changes in public health policy are warranted. O ne of the global health success stories has been the develop­ ment of vaccines. According to the World Health Organization (WHO), we now have vaccines to more than 30 life-threatening infections, which is estimated to prevent 3.5 million to 5 million deaths every year. The recent COVID-19 pandemic showcased vaccine development at its best. Vaccines against SARS-CoV-2 were developed at record speed, facilitated by new technologies such as the mRNA vaccine platform and spurred by the wealth of existing knowledge of virology, vac­ cinology and structural biology. However, much has changed since the beginning of the COVID-19 pandemic. In this issue of Nature Immunology, we have commissioned several commentaries from experts across the globe that collectively summarize recent progress and discuss the challenges faced in this field. Although the COVID-19 pandemic high­ lighted the transformative potential of mRNA platforms, gaps remain for global pandemic preparedness according to Florian Kram­ mer. In his Comment, Krammer argues that there is a need to advance mucosal vaccines to improve protection against respiratory infection and transmission, and to develop more broadly protective vaccines that target entire virus families. In addition, strengthen­ ing regulatory policy and sustaining support for basic research on emerging pathogens is needed. However, the current US adminis­ tration has adopted measures that weaken vaccine development in the USA by the deci­ sion to withdraw federal support for mRNA vaccine develop...

与两个月常规免疫暂时相关的急性免疫性血小板减少性紫癜罕见病例。

A RARE CASE OF ACUTE IMMUNE THROMBOCYTOPENIC PURPURA TEMPORALLY ASSOCIATED WITH ROUTINE TWO-MONTH IMMUNIZATION.
作者： Menahil Asdaque, Dawer Rizwan
摘要中文翻译背景:免疫性血小板减少性紫癜是血小板缺乏的原因之一。尽管 疫苗接种,特别是 MMR,已被报道为潜在的时间触发因素,但常规婴儿免疫接种后的 ITP 非常罕见。病例摘要:我们描述了一名先前健康的 2 个月大婴儿的病例,他被父母带到急诊室,抱怨三天前开始出现扩散的非变白的点状皮疹和瘀伤,最初在腿部更加突出,后来向胸部蔓延。婴儿足月出生,通过自然阴道分娩,需要真空辅助,分娩时没有观察到出血和瘀伤,出生时不需要任何入院。就诊的婴儿临床表现良好,能够耐受经口喂养,没有发烧史,也没有既往病毒性疾病史;也没有任何外伤史。值得注意的是,该婴儿于 15 天前接受了童年常规 疫苗接种,包括 6 合 1、PCV、Men B 和口服轮状病毒。这位母亲还注意到 Men B 注射部位有瘀伤和肿胀。方法:在详细询问病史后到达急诊室后,进行了血液检查,初步检查显示孤立性严重血小板减少症,血小板计数为 6 × 109/L,血红蛋白为 10.5 g/dL,白细胞计数为 9.4 × 109/L。血涂片显示血小板减少,没有发现血小板聚集的证据。最初的胶片显示中性粒细胞减少症,中性粒细胞绝对计数为 0.74 x 109/L,偶尔有反应性淋巴细胞。没有看到母细胞。还进行了凝血研究,结果正常。他的颅骨超声检查也报告为正常。扩展呼吸道病毒检测结果呈阴性,爱泼斯坦-巴尔病毒和巨细胞病毒检测也呈阴性。随后与 paeds 血液学团队对该患者进行了讨论,并制定了急性免疫性血小板减少症的 诊断。考虑到血小板计数确实较低,计划给予IVIG(静脉注射免疫球蛋白),婴儿接受了一剂静脉注射免疫球蛋白(IVIG)。此后患者病情稳定,复查FBC(全血细胞计数)显示血小板计数有改善趋势;随着血小板计数开始改善,血液学建议他出院并每周进行血小板计数。治疗 两周后血小板计数改善至 68 × 109/L,随后在五个月的随访中正常化至 508 × 109/L。常规免疫接种暂时推迟至血小板恢复后,随后根据国家免疫接种计划恢复。家人也放心,未来的 麻疹、腮腺炎和风疹 (MMR) 疫苗接种 没有禁忌症。结论:该病例强调了识别 ITP 的重要性,该儿童在其他方面表现良好,但出现瘀伤和瘀点,血涂片显示孤立性血小板减少症。同样重要的是,我们要排除其他替代原因,并在与血液学团队讨论后立即使用 IVIG 治疗 ITP。此外,密切的血液学随访导致血小板完全恢复并安全恢复常规儿童免疫接种。

最初的胶片显示中性粒细胞减少症,中性粒细胞绝对计数为 0.74 x 109/L,偶尔有反应性淋巴细胞。

A RARE CASE OF ACUTE IMMUNE THROMBOCYTOPENIC PURPURA TEMPORALLY ASSOCIATED WITH ROUTINE TWO-MONTH IMMUNIZATION.

Authors: Menahil Asdaque, Dawer Rizwan
Original abstract (English)
Background: Immune thrombocytopenic purpura is one of the causes of platelet deficiency. Although vaccinations, particularly MMR, have been reported as a potential temporal trigger, ITP following routine infant immunisation is very rare. Case Summary: We describe the case of a previously healthy 2-month-old infant who was brought to the Emergency Department by his parents with complaints of a spreading non-blanching petechial rash and bruising, which started three days back, initially more prominent over the legs and later on spreading towards the chest. The baby was born at full term by spontaneous vaginal delivery requiring vacuum assistance and no bleeding and bruising were observed at the time of delivery and he didn’t require any admission at the time of birth. The baby on presentation was clinically well, was tolerating oral feeds, there was no history of fever, and no history of preceding viral illness; there was also no history of any trauma. Of note, the infant received his routine childhood vaccinations, including 6 in 1, PCV, Men B, and oral rotavirus 15 days ago. The mother also noted bruising and swelling at the Men B injection site. Method: Upon arrival to the ED after a detailed history, blood tests were done, and initial investigations demonstrated isolated severe thrombocytopenia, with a platelet count of 6 × 109/L, hemoglobin of 10.5 g/dL, and white blood cell count of 9.4 × 109/L. Blood film showed thrombocytopenia with no evidence of platelet clumping noted. The initial film showed neutropenia with an absolute neutrophil count of 0.74 x 109/L and occasional reactive lymphocytes. No blast cells were seen. Coagulation studies were also done and were normal. His cranial ultrasonography was also reported as normal. The extended respiratory viral panel was negative, Epstein–Barr virus, and cytomegalovirus testing were negative as well. The patient was then discussed with the paeds hematology team and a diagnosis of acute immune thrombocytopenia was made. Considering the really low platelet count, it was planned to give IVIG (intravenous immunoglobulin), and the infant received one dose of intravenous immunoglobulin (IVIG). The patient remained stable afterward, and repeat FBC (full blood count) showed an improving trend in the platelet count; as the platelet count started to improve, hematology advised discharging him and doing weekly platelet counts. Platelet counts improved to 68 × 109/L two weeks after treatment and subsequently normalised to 508 × 109/L at five months' follow-up. Routine immunisations were temporarily deferred until platelet recovery and were later resumed according to the national immunisation schedule. The family was also reassured that future measles, mumps and rubella (MMR) vaccination was not contraindicated. Conclusion: This case highlights the importance of recognizing ITP in an otherwise well-appearing child who presents with bruising and petechiae with a blood film showing isolated thrombocytopenia. It is also important that we exclude other alternative causes and promptly treat ITP with IVIG after discussion with the hematology team. Also, close haematology follow-up resulted in complete platelet recovery and safe resumption of routine childhood immunisations.

1985-2025 年土耳其儿童 疫苗接种 覆盖率的国家和地区趋势:生态时间序列研究

National and Regional Trends in Childhood Vaccination Coverage in Türkiye, 1985–2025: An Ecological Time Series Study
作者： Mithat Temizer
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:全国平均水平可能掩盖了儿童 疫苗接种 的区域下降。我描述了截至 2025 年土耳其及其地区发布的行政覆盖范围的趋势。方法:白喉-百日咳-破伤风第三剂 (DPT3)、卡介苗、乙型肝炎第三剂 (HBV3)、含 麻疹 的第一剂 (MCV1) 和肺炎球菌结合物 疫苗 (PCV) 的覆盖范围摘自卫生部出版物。结合 Bonferroni 调整的连接点回归应用于 2002-2025 年的 12 个 NUTS-1 区域,并在 2009-2025 年、2019-2025 年和替代规范中重复。结果:到 2025 年,DPT3 的全国覆盖率为 94%,BCG 的全国覆盖率为 93%,MCV1 和 PCV 加强疫苗的全国覆盖率为 92%。区域指标急剧上升,直到 2006 年至 2012 年的连接点,这些年份与时间表和报告变化重叠。后来的斜率大多为负;在主要模型中,2009-2025 年有 6 次 DPT3 或 MCV1 下降达到调整后的阈值,全部位于安纳托利亚中部、安纳托利亚西部和黑海地区,其他窗口中没有一次。在各种规格下,安纳托利亚中部和黑海西部的 MCV1 持续下降。BCG 在同一地区有所下降;所有 PCV 助推器斜率均为负,没有达到阈值。2013 年至 2025 年期间,报告覆盖率至少 95% 的单位从 DPT3 的 81 个单位中的 80 个下降到 32 个,MCV1 的单位从 75 个下降到 19 个;2018 年调查 DPT3 估计值比行政值低 19 个百分点。结论:已发表的报道已趋于稳定,在中部和黑海地区,缓慢下降,MCV1 的报道最为一致。它与真实吸收率的联系尚不确定。这些地区需要积极召回、全国范围调查和分母审计。

National and Regional Trends in Childhood Vaccination Coverage in Türkiye, 1985–2025: An Ecological Time Series Study

Authors: Mithat Temizer
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: National averages may conceal regional declines in childhood vaccination. I described trends in administrative coverage published for Türkiye and its regions through 2025. Methods: Coverage of the third diphtheria–pertussis–tetanus dose (DPT3), BCG, third hepatitis B dose (HBV3), first measles-containing dose (MCV1) and pneumococcal conjugate vaccine (PCV) was extracted from Ministry of Health publications. Joinpoint regression with Bonferroni adjustment was applied to the 12 NUTS-1 regions for 2002–2025 and repeated for 2009–2025, 2019–2025 and alternative specifications. Results: In 2025, national coverage was 94% for DPT3, 93% for BCG and 92% for MCV1 and the PCV booster. Regional indicators rose steeply until joinpoints in 2006–2012, years that overlap schedule and reporting changes. Later slopes were mostly negative; in the principal models, six DPT3 or MCV1 declines met the adjusted threshold in 2009–2025, all in Central Anatolia, Western Anatolia and the Black Sea regions, and none in the other windows. MCV1 declines in Central Anatolia and Western Black Sea persisted under every specification. BCG declined in the same regions; all PCV booster slopes were negative, none meeting the threshold. Units reporting at least 95% coverage fell from 80 to 32 of 81 for DPT3 and from 75 to 19 for MCV1 during 2013–2025; the 2018 survey DPT3 estimate was 19 points below the administrative value. Conclusions: Published coverage has plateaued and, in central and Black Sea regions, declined slowly, most consistently for MCV1. Its link to true uptake is uncertain. Active recall in these regions, a national coverage survey and a denominator audit are needed.

Core methods

SAKTI:开发集成健康分析仪表板以支持斯勒曼地区卫生办公室的数据驱动决策

SAKTI: DEVELOPMENT OF AN INTEGRATED HEALTH ANALYTICS DASHBOARD TO SUPPORT DATA-DRIVEN DECISION-MAKING AT THE SLEMAN DISTRICT HEALTH OFFICE
作者： Kayana Zeta Kuncoro, Nabila Fikriyatun Nisa, Sheira Dwifa Refinda, Annisa Qurrota A’yun, Ambar Widhayatun, Dian Budi Santoso
摘要中文翻译目标:本研究旨在开发一个名为 SAKTI 的基于网络的综合健康分析仪表板原型,SAKTI 是 Sistem Analitik Kesehatan Terintegrasi(综合健康分析系统)的缩写,作为 Sleman 地区卫生办公室的决策支持工具。方法:该系统是使用系统开发生命周期(SDLC)方法和瀑布模型开发的,包括需求分析、系统设计、实施和初始测试。原型中使用的初始数据集包括斯勒曼县 2023-2025 年期间的三个优先健康指标:疾病预防和控制部门的登革出血热 (DHF) 和 麻疹,以及公共卫生部门的发育迟缓。结果:本研究制作了一个基于网络的 SAKTI 仪表板原型,该原型采用模块化架构,旨在代表斯勒曼地区卫生办公室的四个主要部门:疾病预防和控制、公共卫生、卫生服务和卫生资源。在最初的开发阶段,主动数据实施主要集中在疾病预防控制和公共卫生两个模块,而卫生服务和卫生资源模块则准备为集成就绪模块。仪表板具有双因素身份验证、趋势图、分布图、人口统计可视化和跨部门汇总表。由此产生的可视化效果有效地说明了指标动态的差异,包括登革出血热病例的增加、麻疹 阳性率的下降以及发育迟缓的 麻疹 的减少以及区域间差异。新颖性:SAKTI 作为综合健康仪表板的概念验证,为加强地区卫生办公室级别的数据驱动决策奠定了初步基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在开发一个名为 SAKTI 的基于网络的综合健康分析仪表板原型,SAKTI 是 Sistem Analitik Kesehatan Terintegrasi(综合健康分析系统)的缩写,作为 Sleman 地区卫生办公室的决策支持工具。
设计与对象
方法:该系统是使用系统开发生命周期(SDLC)方法和瀑布模型开发的,包括需求分析、系统设计、实施和初始测试。
核心方法
原型中使用的初始数据集包括斯勒曼县 2023-2025 年期间的三个优先健康指标:疾病预防和控制部门的登革出血热 (DHF) 和 麻疹,以及公共卫生部门的发育迟缓。
主要结果
这项研究制作了一个基于网络的 SAKTI 仪表板原型,该原型具有模块化架构,旨在代表斯勒曼地区卫生办公室的四个主要部门:疾病预防和控制、公共卫生、卫生服务和卫生资源。
解释与创新
仪表板具有双因素身份验证、趋势图、分布图、人口统计可视化和跨部门汇总表。
科研与公卫意义
由此产生的可视化效果有效地说明了指标动态的差异,包括登革出血热病例的增加、麻疹 阳性率的下降以及发育迟缓的 麻疹 的减少以及区域间差异。
局限与证据强度
新颖性:SAKTI 作为综合健康仪表板的概念验证,为加强地区卫生办公室级别的数据驱动决策奠定了初步基础。

SAKTI: DEVELOPMENT OF AN INTEGRATED HEALTH ANALYTICS DASHBOARD TO SUPPORT DATA-DRIVEN DECISION-MAKING AT THE SLEMAN DISTRICT HEALTH OFFICE

Authors: Kayana Zeta Kuncoro, Nabila Fikriyatun Nisa, Sheira Dwifa Refinda, Annisa Qurrota A’yun, Ambar Widhayatun, Dian Budi Santoso
Original abstract (English)
Objective: This study aimed to develop a web-based integrated health analytics dashboard prototype named SAKTI, an acronym for Sistem Analitik Kesehatan Terintegrasi (Integrated Health Analytics System), as a decision-support tool at the Sleman District Health Office. Method: The system was developed using the System Development Life Cycle (SDLC) approach with the Waterfall model, encompassing requirement analysis, system design, implementation, and initial testing. The initial dataset used in the prototype included three priority health indicators in Sleman Regency for the 2023–2025 period: dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) and measles in the Disease Prevention and Control Department, and stunting in the Public Health Department. Results: This study produced a web-based SAKTI dashboard prototype with a modular architecture designed to represent four main departments at the Sleman District Health Office: Disease Prevention and Control, Public Health, Health Services, and Health Resources. In the initial development stage, active data implementation focused on two modules—Disease Prevention and Control and Public Health—while the Health Services and Health Resources modules were prepared as integration-ready modules. The dashboard features two-factor authentication, trend charts, distribution maps, demographic visualizations, and cross-department summary tables. The resulting visualizations effectively illustrated differences in indicator dynamics, including a rise in dengue hemorrhagic fever cases, a decline in the measles positivity rate, and a decrease in stunting prevalence alongside inter-area disparities. Novelty: SAKTI serves as a proof-of-concept for an integrated health dashboard that provides an initial foundation for strengthening data-driven decision-making at the district health office level.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to develop a web-based integrated health analytics dashboard prototype named SAKTI, an acronym for Sistem Analitik Kesehatan Terintegrasi (Integrated Health Analytics System), as a decision-support tool at the Sleman District Health Office.
Design and population
Method: The system was developed using the System Development Life Cycle (SDLC) approach with the Waterfall model, encompassing requirement analysis, system design, implementation, and initial testing.
Core methods
The initial dataset used in the prototype included three priority health indicators in Sleman Regency for the 2023–2025 period: dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) and measles in the Disease Prevention and Control Department, and stunting in the Public Health Department.
Main results
This study produced a web-based SAKTI dashboard prototype with a modular architecture designed to represent four main departments at the Sleman District Health Office: Disease Prevention and Control, Public Health, Health Services, and Health Resources.
Interpretation and novelty
The dashboard features two-factor authentication, trend charts, distribution maps, demographic visualizations, and cross-department summary tables.
Scientific and public-health significance
The resulting visualizations effectively illustrated differences in indicator dynamics, including a rise in dengue hemorrhagic fever cases, a decline in the measles positivity rate, and a decrease in stunting prevalence alongside inter-area disparities.
Limitations and evidence strength
Novelty: SAKTI serves as a proof-of-concept for an integrated health dashboard that provides an initial foundation for strengthening data-driven decision-making at the district health office level.

索马里阿夫戈耶医院 6-59 个月儿童营养不良:一项基于机构的横断面研究

Undernutrition among children aged 6–59 months attending Afgooye Hospital, Somalia: a facility-based cross-sectional study
作者： Yusuf F, Hussein A, Elmi A, Ahmed R, Hassan A, Ahmed H, Muhumed S, Ibrahim K
摘要中文翻译我们描述了阿夫戈耶医院 6-59 个月大儿童的人体测量营养不良情况。方法在这项基于设施的横断面研究(2025 年 2 月至 3 月)中,连续招募了参加生长监测的儿童。他们支持加强基于设施的筛查和护理,以及记录流离失所状况的代表性、季节性重复的社区 监测。关键词:阿夫戈耶、儿童营养、基于设施的研究、MUAC、索马里、发育迟缓、营养不良、消瘦 2.材料和方法 2.1.设计和设置我们于 2025 年 2 月至 3 月在阿夫戈耶医院进行了一项基于设施的横断面研究,该医院距阿夫戈耶医院约 30 公里摩加迪沙,提供妇幼保健服务,包括生长监测。该研究描述了参加这项服务的儿童的特征;它的目的不是为了估计社区或地区 患病率.2.2.参与者 6-59 个月到医院接受生长监测的儿童符合资格,并且护理人员给予知情同意。研究记录没有具体说明已经参加营养计划、因急性营养不良转诊或住院的儿童是否符合资格,或者是否评估双侧凹陷性水肿;2.3. 样本量和招募使用单一比例公式,置信度为 95%,绝对精度为 5%,预期 患病率 为 50% [当不存在可靠的局部估计时,最大化 p(1 − p)],给 384 名儿童;未答复者增加 10%,目标为 422。摘要背景 儿童营养不良仍然是索马里的一个主要公共卫生问题,冲突、气候冲击、流离失所和粮食不安全给健康和 WASH 服务带来压力。国家调查为规划提供信息,但不...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估索马里阿夫戈耶医院 6 至 59 个月大儿童营养不良的 患病率,为公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
设计与对象
2025 年 2 月至 3 月期间,在索马里阿夫戈耶医院开展了一项基于机构的横断面研究,涉及 422 名 6-59 个月大的儿童。
核心方法
该研究使用单一比例公式计算样本量,置信度为 95%,绝对精度为 5%,预期 患病率 为 50%。通过访谈员管理的问卷收集数据,并根据 疫苗接种 卡和医疗记录验证护理人员的报告。
主要结果
在 422 名儿童中,94.3% (95% CI 91.7–96.1) 发育迟缓,55.9% 严重发育迟缓,79.9% (95% CI 75.8–83.4) 按身高体重 Z 值消瘦,39.1% (95% CI 34.6–43.8) 根据 MUAC 患有急性营养不良。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,该诊所 患病率 的营养不良程度很高,这可能表明测量或数据处理问题,或者是经过精心挑选的人群,营养状况的变异性有限。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了阿夫戈耶医院迫切需要有针对性的营养干预措施,并为该地区营养不良的未来研究提供基线数据。
局限与证据强度
非概率抽样方法可能会过度代表有生长或健康问题的儿童,从而限制了普遍性,并且置信区间假设独立抽样,而不考虑选择偏差或测量误差。

Undernutrition among children aged 6–59 months attending Afgooye Hospital, Somalia: a facility-based cross-sectional study

Authors: Yusuf F, Hussein A, Elmi A, Ahmed R, Hassan A, Ahmed H, Muhumed S, Ibrahim K
Original abstract (English)
We describe anthropometric undernutrition among children aged 6–59 months attending Afgooye Hospital.MethodsIn this facility-based cross-sectional study (February–March 2025), children attending growth monitoring were recruited consecutively. They support strengthened facility-based screening and care, and representative, seasonally repeated community surveillance that records displacement status.Keywords: Afgooye, child nutrition, facility-based study, MUAC, Somalia, stunting, undernutrition, wasting 2.Materials and methods2.1.Design and settingWe conducted a facility-based cross-sectional study from February to March 2025 at Afgooye Hospital, about 30 km from Mogadishu, which provides maternal and child health services including growth monitoring. The study characterizes children attending this service; it was not designed to estimate community or district prevalence.2.2.ParticipantsChildren aged 6–59 months attending the hospital for growth monitoring were eligible, and caregivers gave informed consent. The study records do not specify whether children already enrolled in a nutrition programme, referred for acute malnutrition or admitted as inpatients were eligible, or whether bilateral pitting oedema was assessed; these gaps are acknowledged as limitations.2.3.Sample size and recruitmentThe single-proportion formula was used with 95% confidence, 5% absolute precision and an expected prevalence of 50% [which maximizes p(1 − p) when no reliable local estimate exists], giving 384 children; adding 10% for non-response gave a target of 422. AbstractBackgroundChild undernutrition remains a major public-health problem in Somalia, where conflict, climate shocks, displacement and food insecurity strain health and WASH services. National surveys inform planning but do not de...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the prevalence of undernutrition among children aged 6–59 months attending Afgooye Hospital, Somalia, to inform public health interventions.
Design and population
A facility-based cross-sectional study was conducted at Afgooye Hospital, Somalia, between February and March 2025, involving 422 children aged 6–59 months.
Core methods
The study used a single-proportion formula to calculate the sample size with 95% confidence, 5% absolute precision, and an expected prevalence of 50%. Data were collected via an interviewer-administered questionnaire, and caregiver reports were verified against vaccination cards and medical records.
Main results
Among the 422 children, 94.3% (95% CI 91.7–96.1) were stunted, 55.9% severely stunted, 79.9% (95% CI 75.8–83.4) were wasted by weight-for-height Z-score, and 39.1% (95% CI 34.6–43.8) had acute malnutrition by MUAC.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a high prevalence of undernutrition in this clinic cohort, which may indicate measurement or data-handling issues, or a highly selected population with limited variability in nutritional status.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the urgent need for targeted nutrition interventions in Afgooye Hospital and provide baseline data for future studies on undernutrition in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The non-probability sampling approach may over-represent children with growth or health concerns, limiting generalizability, and the confidence intervals assume independent sampling without accounting for selection bias or measurement error.

Sarampión: de la eliminación al rebrote.文学修订与实际建议

Sarampión: de la eliminación al rebrote. Revisión de la literatura y recomendaciones actuales
作者： Alexandro Bonifáz, Jesica Naanous Rayek, Ana P. Landeta-Sa, Ana Cecilia Carbajal-César, Alejandra Margarita Guerrero-Villalpando, Mariana Velez-Pintado
摘要中文翻译病毒性传染,可以通过疫苗和义务通知来预防,这代表了公共健康问题。Su agente causal es elvirus del sarampión (familia Paramyxoviridae),de serotipo único,Transmissionido por via número reproductivo básico de 12 a 18. Lapresentación clínica clásica incluye un prodromo de fiebre,tos,coriza y conjuntivitis,seguido de las manchas de Koplik y un exantema 斑丘疹头尾部。诊断可确认中间血清学(IgM 特异),反应是多聚体或培养病毒的反应。El tratamiento es basicmente de soporte;在儿科患者中,维生素 A 可以将死亡率降低 62%,这是儿童组 OMS 推荐的。Las complicaciones en aproximadamente el 30% de los casos;常见的疾病有腹泻 (8%) 和中耳炎 (7-9%),严重的并发症包括肺炎、腹泻、阿古达脑炎、感染性阿古达脑炎和感染性阿古达脑炎、全脑炎、硬化性亚古达脑炎。Las poblaciones más mus son los menores de 5 años,los Adultos juvenes sin in previa,las embarazadas y los inmunocomprometidos.三重真空(风疹、风疹和腮腺炎)可达到 97% 的功效,并且需要 93% 的免疫能力。在墨西哥,2025-2026 年期间,有 12,000 起确诊病例和 33 起失业事件发生,这对市长造成了重大影响。72 小时内三重真空的轮廓暴露,6 天内免疫球蛋白的暴露,表明易感人群易受影响。世界协调、真空隔离营和市长治安诊所为法国传播提供基本保障。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
病毒性传染,可以通过疫苗和义务通知来预防,这代表了公共健康问题。
设计与对象
副粘病毒科(Paramyxoviridae)病毒的致病原因是,通过 aérea con un número reproductivo básico de 12 a 18 传播。
核心方法
经典的临床演示包括纤维性前兆、结膜炎、结膜炎、科普利克的症状和头尾部黄斑丘疹的症状。
主要结果
诊断可确认中间血清学(IgM 特异),反应是多聚体或培养病毒的反应。
解释与创新
El tratamiento es basicmente de soporte;在儿科患者中,维生素 A 可以将死亡率降低 62%,这是儿童组 OMS 推荐的。
科研与公卫意义
Las complicaciones en aproximadamente el 30% de los casos;常见的疾病有腹泻 (8%) 和中耳炎 (7-9%),严重的并发症包括肺炎、腹泻、阿古达脑炎、感染性阿古达脑炎和感染性阿古达脑炎、全脑炎、硬化性亚古达脑炎。
局限与证据强度
最容易受影响的人群是5岁以下的儿童、未接种疫苗的年轻人、孕妇和免疫抑制患者。

Sarampión: de la eliminación al rebrote. Revisión de la literatura y recomendaciones actuales

Authors: Alexandro Bonifáz, Jesica Naanous Rayek, Ana P. Landeta-Sa, Ana Cecilia Carbajal-César, Alejandra Margarita Guerrero-Villalpando, Mariana Velez-Pintado
Original abstract (English)
El sarampión es una enfermedad viral altamente contagiosa, prevenible por vacunación y de notificación obligatoria, que representa un problema de salud pública vigente. Su agente causal es el virus del sarampión (familia Paramyxoviridae), de serotipo único, transmitido por vía aérea con un número reproductivo básico de 12 a 18. La presentación clínica clásica incluye un pródromo de fiebre, tos, coriza y conjuntivitis, seguido de las manchas de Koplik y un exantema maculopapular cefalocaudal. El diagnóstico se confirma mediante serología (IgM específica), reacción en cadena de la polimerasa o cultivo viral. El tratamiento es fundamentalmente de soporte; en pacientes pediátricos, la vitamina A reduce la mortalidad hasta un 62%, por lo que está recomendada por la OMS en este grupo de pacientes. Las complicaciones ocurren en aproximadamente el 30% de los casos; las más frecuentes son diarrea (8%) y otitis media (7-9%), y las complicaciones graves incluyen neumonía, diarrea, encefalitis aguda, encefalomielitis aguda posinfecciosa y, a largo plazo, panencefalitis esclerosante subaguda. Las poblaciones más vulnerables son los menores de 5 años, los adultos jóvenes sin inmunización previa, las embarazadas y los inmunocomprometidos. La vacuna triple vírica (sarampión, rubeola y parotiditis) tiene una eficacia del 97% tras dos dosis y requiere una cobertura poblacional superior al 93% para alcanzar la inmunidad de rebaño. En México, la cobertura vacunal ha disminuido significativamente, lo que derivó en más de 12,000 casos confirmados y más de 33 defunciones durante el brote de 2025-2026, afectando mayoritariamente a niños no vacunados. La profilaxis posexposición con la vacuna triple vírica en un plazo de 72 horas, o con inmunoglobulina en un plazo de 6 días, está indicada para personas susceptibles de alto riesgo. La coordinación mundial, las campañas de vacunación sostenidas y una mayor vigilancia clínica siguen siendo fundamentales para frenar la transmisión.

从卓越到证据再到基于智能的医学:优化人工智能以打破急诊科的教条

From eminence to evidence to intelligence-based medicine: optimising artificial intelligence to disrupt dogma in emergency departments
作者： Tran V
摘要中文翻译1.简介医学认识论是关于如何获取、验证医学知识并最终应用于患者护理的哲学研究,在整个人类历史中经历了深刻的转变 (1)。这一新兴阶段的定义是人工智能 (AI) 与日常护理的深度融合,它将知识的轨迹从静态的、人口层面的证据转变为动态的、超个性化的见解 (5)。表 1 医学认识论的演变。认识论时代维度卓越证据情报简介20 世纪末之前 20 世纪末(1990 年代)21 世纪初(2020年代)认知权威专家:资历、临床直觉、历史先例和积累的个人经验。方法:科学经验主义、严谨的研究设计和统计意义。算法:模式识别、概率建模和高维数据分析知识来源临床知识、轶事、传统文本、非结构化和专家意见随机对照试验、系统评价、荟萃分析和临床指南定期收集数据、同行评审、灰色、专业和流行文献引入时的优势决策确定性、标准化培训和实践客观、可重复的护理、以患者为中心、医生一致和评估个性化护理、整合大量信息固有限制/脆弱性不灵活、教条主义、非评估性人口偏见、渎职、劳动密集型过程盲目、非评估性、自动化偏见尽管医学领域存在这些范式转变就认识论而言,医学教条的潜在威胁仍然是所有三个时代的持续挑战(6、7)。摘要医学认识论的进化来自无懈可击的权威...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决将人工智能 (AI) 集成到急诊科时的现代化数字教条风险,强调需要防止算法偏差并确保临床护理始终以经过验证的证据为基础。
设计与对象
该研究探讨了医学认识论的 进化 以及将人工智能融入临床实践的挑战,重点关注急诊科和更广泛的医疗保健系统。
核心方法
该研究利用历史和当代的例子来评估人工智能部署的风险,包括机器学习模型的黑匣子性质以及自动化偏差和认知漂移的可能性。
主要结果
研究结果表明,在急诊医学评估的荟萃分析中,只有一小部分(2.8%)符合高质量证据的严格标准,并且人工智能系统容易受到隐性偏见和过时的临床启发法的影响。
解释与创新
作者解释说,人工智能与急诊部门的整合必须受到透明框架的约束,以防止数字教条的出现,从而提供一种将计算能力与动态、经过验证的证据结合起来的新颖方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明需要对急诊医学中的人工智能系统进行严格的监督和治理,以确保它们不会延续历史偏见或错误,从而保障患者护理和临床决策。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于历史和一般观察,而不是具体的、以目标为中心的证据,这限制了其结论的力度,并需要进一步的有针对性的研究。

From eminence to evidence to intelligence-based medicine: optimising artificial intelligence to disrupt dogma in emergency departments

Authors: Tran V
Original abstract (English)
1.IntroductionMedical epistemology, the philosophical study of how medical knowledge is acquired, validated, and ultimately applied to patient care, has undergone profound transformation throughout human history (1). Defined by the deep integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into everyday care, this emerging phase shifts the locus of knowledge from static, population-level evidence to dynamic, hyper-personalised insights (5).Table 1Evolution of medical epistemology.Epistemological eraDimensionEminenceEvidenceIntelligenceIntroductionPrior to late 20th centuryLate 20th century (1990s)Early 21st century (2020s)Epistemic authorityThe Expert: Seniority, clinical intuition, historical precedence, and accumulated personal experience.The Method: Scientific empiricism, rigorous study design and statistical significance.The Algorithm: Pattern recognition, probabilistic modelling, and high-dimensional data analysisSources of knowledgeClinical lore, anecdotes, traditional texts, unstructured and expert opinionRandomised controlled trials, systematic reviews, meta-analyses, and clinical guidelinesRoutinely collected data, peer reviewed, grey, professional and popular literatureStrengths at time of introductionDecisional certainty, standardised training and practisesObjective, reproducible care, patient focused, physician aligned and evaluativePersonalised care, consolidation of vast amounts of informationInherent Limits/FragilityInflexible, dogmatic, non-evaluativePopulation bias, malfeasance, labour intensiveprocess-blind, non-evaluative, automation biasDespite these paradigm shifts in medical epistemology, the underlying threat of medical dogma remains a persistent challenge across all three eras (6, 7). View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the risk of modernised, digital dogma in the integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into emergency departments, highlighting the need to prevent algorithmic biases and ensure clinical care remains grounded in validated evidence.
Design and population
The study examined the evolution of medical epistemology and the challenges of integrating AI into clinical practice, focusing on emergency departments and the broader healthcare system.
Core methods
The study utilised historical and contemporary examples to evaluate the risks of AI deployment, including the black box nature of machine learning models and the potential for automation bias and epistemic drift.
Main results
The findings indicate that only a marginal proportion (2.8%) of evaluated meta-analyses in emergency medicine meet stringent criteria for high-quality evidence, and that AI systems are vulnerable to implicit biases and outdated clinical heuristics.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the integration of AI into emergency departments must be governed by transparent frameworks to prevent the emergence of digital dogma, offering a novel approach to align computational power with dynamic, validated evidence.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the need for rigorous oversight and governance of AI systems in emergency medicine to ensure they do not perpetuate historical biases or errors, thereby safeguarding patient care and clinical decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on historical and general observations rather than specific, target-focused evidence, which limits the strength of its conclusions and necessitates further targeted research.

Measles in adults: emergency department identification and management.

Authors: Bobbi-Jo Lowie, John David Gatz
Original abstract (English)
Increasing numbers of measles outbreaks in the United States have renewed the need for rapid recognition and management in emergency departments. Although adult presentations vary, emergency clinicians must recognize the classic prodrome and identify patients at risk for severe disease. Prompt airborne precautions, appropriate diagnostic testing, and timely postexposure prophylaxis are essential to limit healthcare-associated and community transmission. This review summarizes the clinical features, diagnosis, complications, and evidence-based management of measles in adults in the emergency department.

KINDERGARTEN NONMEDICAL EXEMPTIONS AND CLUSTERED MEASLES CASES ACROSS US COUNTIES DURING THE 2025 RESURGENCE

Authors: Y. Tony Yang
Original abstract (English)
Background: The 2025 US measles resurgence was concentrated in communities with low vaccination coverage. Aggregate national and state measles-mumps-rubella (MMR) coverage can mask local susceptibility: pockets of unvaccinated children persist where average coverage appears adequate. School-entry nonmedical exemptions (NMEs) are collected routinely and may capture vaccination behavior and clustering of susceptible children. We examined whether county-level kindergarten NME rates were associated with reporting at least 3 measles cases in 2025, independently of average county MMR coverage. Methods: Cross-sectional ecological study of US counties. Confirmed cases reported January 1 to December 31, 2025, came from the Johns Hopkins University US Measles Tracker. Kindergarten MMR coverage and NME rates (school year 2023-2024 or, if unavailable, 2022- 2023) came from public state reports and published county-level compilations. The prespecified outbreak proxy was 3 or more versus 0-2 reported cases per county, a count threshold without verified epidemiologic linkage. A secondary, exploratory outcome was total case burden among counties with 3 or more cases. Firth penalized logistic regression estimated odds ratios (ORs) for occurrence; negative binomial regression with a log county-population offset and state-clustered robust standard errors estimated incidence rate ratios (IRRs) for burden. Models adjusted for percentage foreign-born, metropolitan status, population density, percentage uninsured, college education, 2024 presidential vote share, and primary care physician supply; estimates are per 1-percentage-point increase. Sensitivity analyses varied the threshold and adjustment set and examined spatial dependence, international airport proximity, state exclusions, and modified Poisson estimation. Only aggregate, publicly available data were analyzed; the study was exempt from institutional review. Results: The sample comprised 1914 counties in 30 states (237 million residents, 71.6% of the US population), capturing 1821 of 2214 reported 2025 cases (82.2%); 66 counties (3.4%) met the outbreak proxy. Mean (SD) MMR coverage was 91.1% (7.2%) and mean NME rate 4.58% (4.37%). In the joint model, each 1-percentage-point higher NME rate was associated with higher odds of meeting the proxy (OR 1.16; 95% CI 1.09-1.24; P<.001), whereas average MMR coverage was not (OR 1.01; 95% CI 0.96 1.07; P=.78). The exemption association persisted across thresholds of 1 to 10 or more cases (ORs 1.12-1.17), with reduced adjustment (OR 1.15), airport-proximity adjustment (OR 1.16), and modified Poisson estimation (risk ratio 1.14; 95% CI 1.06-1.23). Residual spatial autocorrelation was present (Moran I 0.0758; P=.001), and adding neighboring counties' exemption rates attenuated the OR to 1.12 (95% CI 1.03-1.20). In the exploratory burden model, the IRR was 1.08 (95% CI 1.00-1.16; P=.046), with confidence intervals crossing the null in several specifications. Conclusions: Across US counties in 2025, kindergarten nonmedical exemption rates were associated with clustered measles case reports independently of average MMR coverage, which carried little additional information once exemptions were considered. Estimates are ecological, not individual-level or causal; the count proxy did not establish epidemiologic linkage, and exposure definitions varied across states. Routinely collected school-entry exemption data may nonetheless complement local knowledge when pediatricians, school health programs, and health departments prioritize school-level review and outbreak preparedness.
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
In the joint model, each 1-percentage-point higher NME rate was associated with higher odds of meeting the proxy (OR 1.16; 95% CI 1.09-1.24; P<.001), whereas average MMR coverage was not (OR 1.01; 95% CI 0.96 1.07; P=.78).
Controversies and gaps

麻疹:从淘汰到复兴。文献综述和当前建议

Measles: from elimination to resurgence. A literature review and current recommendations
作者： Alexandro Bonifáz, Jesica Naanous Rayek, Ana P. Landeta-Sa, Ana Cecilia Carbajal-César, Alejandra Margarita Guerrero-Villalpando, Mariana Velez-Pintado, Alexandro Bonifaz, Jesica Naanous-Rayek, Mariana Vélez-Pintado
摘要中文翻译麻疹 是一种具有高度传染性、可预防的 麻疹 病毒性疾病,需要进行强制流行病学报告,仍然是一个活跃的全球公共卫生威胁。它由 麻疹病毒(副粘病毒科)引起​​,是一种基本繁殖数为 12-18 的单血清型病原体,通过雾化呼吸道飞沫传播。典型的临床表现以发烧、咳嗽、鼻炎和结膜炎等前驱症状开始,随后出现科普利克斑和特征性头尾斑丘疹。诊断 通过特异性 IgM 血清学、聚合酶链式反应或病毒培养来确认。管理层主要是支持性的;补充维生素 A 可使儿童期 死亡率 减少高达 62%,世界卫生组织普遍建议所有确诊儿童都服用维生素 A。大约 30% 的病例会出现并发症;最常见且通常较轻的并发症包括腹泻 (8%) 和中耳炎 (7-9%),而严重并发症包括肺炎、腹泻、急性脑炎、急性感染后 麻疹 脑脊髓炎,以及多年后的 亚急性硬化性全脑炎。最脆弱的人群包括 5 岁以下儿童、未事先接种疫苗的年轻人、孕妇和免疫功能低下的人。MMR 疫苗(麻疹,腮腺炎和风疹)在两次接种后可达到 97% 的疗效,并需要 93% 以上的人群覆盖率才能维持群体免疫。在墨西哥,疫苗接种 发病率下降导致 2025 年至 2026 年 疫苗接种 期间累计确诊病例超过 12,000 例,死亡人数累计超过 33 例,其中主要是未接种疫苗的儿童。对于易感高危个体,需要在 72 小时内使用 MMR 或在 6 天内使用免疫球蛋白进行暴露后预防。全球协调、持续的 疫苗接种 活动和提高临床意识对于遏制 传播 仍然至关重要。本文旨在对 麻疹 进行全面回顾,涵盖相关风险、诊断、治疗 和预防。

Measles: from elimination to resurgence. A literature review and current recommendations

Authors: Alexandro Bonifáz, Jesica Naanous Rayek, Ana P. Landeta-Sa, Ana Cecilia Carbajal-César, Alejandra Margarita Guerrero-Villalpando, Mariana Velez-Pintado, Alexandro Bonifaz, Jesica Naanous-Rayek, Mariana Vélez-Pintado
Original abstract (English)
Measles is a highly contagious, vaccine-preventable viral disease of mandatory epidemiological reporting that remains an active global public health threat. Caused by the measles virus (Paramyxoviridae family), a single-serotype pathogen with a basic reproduction number of 12-18, it is transmitted via aerosolized respiratory droplets. The classic clinical presentation begins with a prodrome of fever, cough, coryza, and conjunctivitis, followed by Koplik’s spots and a characteristic cephalocaudal maculopapular rash. Diagnosis is confirmed through specific IgM serology, polymerase chain reaction or viral culture. Management is primarily supportive; vitamin A supplementation reduces childhood mortality by up to 62% and is universally recommended by the WHO for all diagnosed children. Complications occur in approximately 30% of cases; the most frequent and usually mild include diarrhea (8%) and otitis media (7-9%), while severe complications include pneumonia, diarrhea, acute encephalitis, acute post-infectious measles encephalomyelitis, and, years later, subacute sclerosing panencephalitis. The most vulnerable populations include children under 5, young adults without prior immunization, pregnant women, and immunocompromised individuals. The MMR vaccine (measles, mumps and rubella) achieves 97% efficacy after two doses and requires population coverage above 93% to sustain herd immunity. In Mexico, declining vaccination rates contributed to more than 12,000 confirmed cases and over 33 deaths accumulated during the 2025–2026 outbreak, predominantly in unvaccinated children. Post-exposure prophylaxis with MMR within 72 hours or immunoglobulin within 6 days is indicated for susceptible high-risk individuals. Global coordination, sustained vaccination campaigns, and heightened clinical awareness remain essential to curb transmission. This article was prepared to provide a comprehensive review of measles, covering the associated risks, diagnosis, treatment, and prevention.

Global coordination, sustained vaccination campaigns, and heightened clinical awareness remain essential to curb transmission.

量化低收入和中等收入国家自然灾害暴露与幼儿免疫接种之间的关系。

Quantifying the relationship between exposure to natural disasters and early-childhood immunization in low- and middle-income countries.
作者： Brittany Shea, Joan A. Casey, Ilan Cerna-Turoff, Rodrigo Zepeda-Tello, Jeffrey L. Shaman, Rodrigo Zepeda‐Tello
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
我们利用 2002 年进行的《年轻生命:国际儿童贫困研究》调查中的个人数据,估计了埃塞俄比亚、印度(安得拉邦和特伦甘纳邦)、秘鲁和越南家庭遭受自然灾害的风险与母婴对产前和幼儿期 疫苗接种 的关系。
证据基础与方法
我们建立了混合效应逻辑回归模型,其中自然灾害暴露与 疫苗 类型(产前检查期间≥2剂产前破伤风、产后卡介苗[卡介苗]、产后 麻疹)之间的相互作用,以财富指数和儿童年龄作为固定效应进行控制,并包括国家、哨点地点和个人 ID 的嵌套随机效应。
共识与结论
Exposure to natural disaster was associated with an increased probability of receiving ≥ 2 doses of antenatal tetanus vaccine during antenatal visits of 5.75 percentage points (estimate: 0.0575; 95% CI: 0.0186, 0.0964), and a decreased probability of receiving postnatal BCG vaccine against tuberculosis of 3.11 percentage points (estimate: -0.0311; 95% CI: -0.0576, -0.00455).
争议与缺口
然而,还需要进一步的研究来验证结果并确定因果关系。
科研与实践启示
这项研究发现,接触自然灾害与产前和幼儿期 疫苗接种 相关,这强调了在最近受自然灾害影响的地区加强针对特定 疫苗 的免疫接种工作的重要性。

Quantifying the relationship between exposure to natural disasters and early-childhood immunization in low- and middle-income countries.

Authors: Brittany Shea, Joan A. Casey, Ilan Cerna-Turoff, Rodrigo Zepeda-Tello, Jeffrey L. Shaman, Rodrigo Zepeda‐Tello
Original abstract (English)
Limited research has explored the relationship between exposure to natural hazards and antenatal and early childhood vaccination indicators. We leveraged individual-level data from Young Lives: An International Study of Childhood Poverty surveys conducted in 2002 to estimate the association of household exposure to natural disaster in Ethiopia, India (in the states of Andhra Pradesh and Telangana), Peru, and Vietnam on antenatal and early childhood vaccinations among mother/child pairs. We ran mixed-effects logistic regression models with interaction between exposure to natural disaster and vaccine type (≥ 2 doses of antenatal tetanus during antenatal visits, postnatal BCG [Bacillus Calmette-Guérin], postnatal measles), controlled for wealth index and child age as fixed effects, and included nested random effects with country, sentinel site, and individual ID. We found that the association between exposure to natural disaster and vaccination varied by vaccine type. Exposure to natural disaster was associated with an increased probability of receiving ≥ 2 doses of antenatal tetanus vaccine during antenatal visits of 5.75 percentage points (estimate: 0.0575; 95% CI: 0.0186, 0.0964), and a decreased probability of receiving postnatal BCG vaccine against tuberculosis of 3.11 percentage points (estimate: -0.0311; 95% CI: -0.0576, -0.00455). We did not find a significant association for postnatal measles vaccination (estimate: 0.00647; 95% CI: -0.03, 0.0429). This study finds that exposure to natural disasters is associated with antenatal and early childhood vaccination, underscoring the importance of increasing immunization efforts for specific vaccines in areas recently affected by natural disasters. However, further studies are needed to verify results and establish causation.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
We leveraged individual-level data from Young Lives: An International Study of Childhood Poverty surveys conducted in 2002 to estimate the association of household exposure to natural disaster in Ethiopia, India (in the states of Andhra Pradesh and Telangana), Peru, and Vietnam on antenatal and early childhood vaccinations among mother/child pairs.
Evidence base and method
We ran mixed-effects logistic regression models with interaction between exposure to natural disaster and vaccine type (≥ 2 doses of antenatal tetanus during antenatal visits, postnatal BCG [Bacillus Calmette-Guérin], postnatal measles), controlled for wealth index and child age as fixed effects, and included nested random effects with country, sentinel site, and individual ID.
Consensus and conclusions
Exposure to natural disaster was associated with an increased probability of receiving ≥ 2 doses of antenatal tetanus vaccine during antenatal visits of 5.75 percentage points (estimate: 0.0575; 95% CI: 0.0186, 0.0964), and a decreased probability of receiving postnatal BCG vaccine against tuberculosis of 3.11 percentage points (estimate: -0.0311; 95% CI: -0.0576, -0.00455).
Controversies and gaps
However, further studies are needed to verify results and establish causation.
Research and practice implications
This study finds that exposure to natural disasters is associated with antenatal and early childhood vaccination, underscoring the importance of increasing immunization efforts for specific vaccines in areas recently affected by natural disasters.

原发性免疫缺陷综合征患者造血干细胞移植后预防 疫苗 可预防感染的差距。

GAPS in Protection Against Vaccine-Preventable Infections in Patients Post-Haematopoietic Stem Cell Transplant for Primary Immunodeficiency Syndromes.
作者： Kimberley Chin-Goh, Carol Furness, Radha Gorantla, Nicola Parker, Kuang-Chih Hsiao
摘要中文翻译简介:原发性免疫缺陷 (PID) 综合征患者在造血干细胞移植 (HSCT) 后容易受到 疫苗 可预防的感染。免疫重建后及时重新免疫至关重要。方法:通过机构数据库确定 2018-2025 年在新西兰 Starship 儿童医院进行的用于治疗儿童(年龄≤18 岁)的 HSCT。审查了当地和国家电子病历、临床和实验室数据以及 疫苗接种 记录(新西兰免疫登记册)。HSCT 后再免疫资格标准和审核标准基于新西兰国家儿童癌症网络指南。结果:在接受 236 次 HSCT 的 197 名独特患者中,28 名患者因 PID 而接受 HSCT。如果患者目前因临床原因未清除重新免疫 (n = 2) 或在 HSCT 6 个月内死亡 (n = 4),则患者被排除。本初步报告纳入了 22 名符合重新免疫资格的患者。22 人中的 1 人 (4.5%) 尚未开始重新免疫。重新免疫许可日期和第一个非活体 疫苗 给药之间的中位时间为 64 天(四分位距 [IQR] 9-126 天)。33% 的患者 (7/21) 出现与标准 疫苗接种 序列的偏差。在任何一次访问中,85% (18/21) 的人没有获得所有符合条件的 疫苗。9% (2/22) 在审核时完全了解最新的重新免疫情况。20 名患者有资格接受实时 疫苗 治疗。65% (13/20) 接受了至少一剂含有 麻疹 的 疫苗 (MCV)。最后一次血液制品给药与首次注射 MCV 之间的中位时间为 669 天(IQR 476-766 天)。50% (10/20) 接受了 2 剂 MCV。40% (4/10) 进行了 MCV 后 麻疹 血清学 检查;尽管注射了 2 剂 MCV,但仍有一名患者血清呈阴性。结论:HSCT 后重新免疫计划的完成情况并不理想,在针对 疫苗 可预防感染的保护方面存在差距。重新免疫许可和开始之间存在相当大的延迟。MCV 的摄取和确认血清转化的做法差异很大。

范围与问题
原发性免疫缺陷 (PID) 综合征患者在造血干细胞移植 (HSCT) 后容易受到 疫苗 可预防的感染。免疫重建后及时重新免疫至关重要。麻疹病毒(人麻疹病毒)、历史分类学名称、风疹和 麻疹 疾病是焦点。核心问题是针对 PID 综合征患者进行 HSCT 后预防 疫苗 可预防感染的差距。
证据基础与方法
方法:通过机构数据库确定 2018-2025 年在新西兰 Starship 儿童医院进行的用于治疗儿童(年龄≤18 岁)的 HSCT。审查了当地和国家电子病历、临床和实验室数据以及 疫苗接种 记录(新西兰免疫登记册)。HSCT 后再免疫资格标准和审核标准基于新西兰国家儿童癌症网络指南。
共识与结论
HSCT 后重新免疫计划的完成情况并不理想,导致在针对 疫苗 可预防感染的保护方面存在差距。重新免疫许可和开始之间存在相当大的延迟。MCV 的摄取和确认血清转化的做法差异很大。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据并未报告 麻疹 疫苗 在 HSCT 后 PID 患者中的具体有效性。所提供的证据并未报告 PID 患者 HSCT 后的长期免疫力或血清转化率。所提供的证据没有报告延迟重新免疫对感染率的影响。
科研与实践启示
需要改进 HSCT 后重新免疫计划的遵守情况。需要更好地监测 PID 患者 HSCT 后的 疫苗 摄取和血清转化。需要进一步研究 麻疹 疫苗 在 PID 患者 HSCT 后的最佳时机和有效性。

GAPS in Protection Against Vaccine-Preventable Infections in Patients Post-Haematopoietic Stem Cell Transplant for Primary Immunodeficiency Syndromes.

Authors: Kimberley Chin-Goh, Carol Furness, Radha Gorantla, Nicola Parker, Kuang-Chih Hsiao
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Patients with primary immunodeficiency (PID) syndromes are susceptible to vaccine-preventable infections post-haematopoietic stem cell transplant (HSCT). Timely re-immunisation following immune reconstitution is critical. METHOD: HSCTs conducted 2018-2025 at Starship Children's Hospital (New Zealand) to treat children (age ≤18 years) were identified through the institutional database. Local and national electronic medical records, clinical and laboratory data, and vaccination records (Aotearoa Immunisation Register) were reviewed. Post-HSCT re-immunisation eligibility criteria and audit standards were based on National Child Cancer Network NZ guidelines. RESULTS: Of 197 unique patients who received 236 HSCTs, 28 patients had PID as the reason for HSCT. Patients were excluded if they were not currently cleared for re-immunisation for clinical reasons (n = 2) or died within 6 months of HSCT (n = 4). Twenty-two patients who were eligible for re-immunisation were included for this preliminary report. One of 22 (4.5%) had not commenced re-immunisation. Median time between the date of clearance for re-immunisation and administration of the first non-live vaccine was 64 days (interquartile range [IQR] 9-126 days). Deviation from standard vaccination sequence occurred in 33% of patients (7/21). At any one visit, 85% (18/21) were not given all eligible vaccines. 9% (2/22) were fully up-to-date with re-immunisations at the time of audit. Twenty patients were eligible for live vaccines. 65% (13/20) were given at least one dose of a measles-containing vaccine (MCV). Median time between last blood product administration and first dose of MCV was 669 days (IQR 476-766 days). 50% (10/20) received 2 doses of MCV. 40% (4/10) had post-MCV measles serology checked; one individual remained seronegative despite 2 doses of MCV. CONCLUSION: Completion of re-immunisation schedules post-HSCT is suboptimal, leaving gaps in protection against vaccine-preventable infections. There is considerable delay between clearance for re-immunisation and commencement. Uptake of MCV and the practice of confirming seroconversion is highly variable.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Patients with primary immunodeficiency (PID) syndromes are susceptible to vaccine-preventable infections post-haematopoietic stem cell transplant (HSCT). Timely re-immunisation following immune reconstitution is critical. Measles virus (Morbillivirus hominis), historical taxonomic names, rubeola and measles disease are the focus. The central question is about gaps in protection against vaccine-preventable infections in patients post-HSCT for PID syndromes.
Evidence base and method
METHOD: HSCTs conducted 2018-2025 at Starship Children's Hospital (New Zealand) to treat children (age ≤18 years) were identified through the institutional database. Local and national electronic medical records, clinical and laboratory data, and vaccination records (Aotearoa Immunisation Register) were reviewed. Post-HSCT re-immunisation eligibility criteria and audit standards were based on National Child Cancer Network NZ guidelines.
Consensus and conclusions
Completion of re-immunisation schedules post-HSCT is suboptimal, leaving gaps in protection against vaccine-preventable infections. There is considerable delay between clearance for re-immunisation and commencement. Uptake of MCV and the practice of confirming seroconversion is highly variable.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report on the specific effectiveness of measles vaccines in PID patients post-HSCT. The supplied evidence does not report on the long-term immunity or seroconversion rates in PID patients post-HSCT. The supplied evidence does not report on the impact of delayed re-immunisation on infection rates.
Research and practice implications
There is a need for improved adherence to re-immunisation schedules post-HSCT. There is a need for better monitoring of vaccine uptake and seroconversion in PID patients post-HSCT. There is a need for further research on the optimal timing and effectiveness of measles vaccines in PID patients post-HSCT.

病毒病原体作为全球健康威胁:传播性、致死性和大流行潜力的回顾

Viral Pathogens as Global Health Threats: Review of Transmissibility, Lethality, and Pandemic Potential
作者： Jonathan A. Coffman, Franklin Garcia-Godoy, Peter E. Murray, F. García-Godoy
摘要中文翻译病毒病原体的传播性、致死性以及引起广泛流行病或大流行的可能性差异很大。例如,麻疹 具有极高的传染性,但在能够获得现代医疗护理的人群中病死率相对较低。相反,狂犬病、尼帕病毒、埃博拉病毒和马尔堡病毒可引起严重疾病,病死率高,但通常表现出有限的持续人际传播 传播。因此,最大的全球威胁不一定是最具传染性或致命性的病毒,而是那些结合了有效的 传播、临床严重性、人畜共患 储存宿主、进化适应性和有限的预防或治疗选择的病毒。COVID-19大流行说明了风险因素的这种融合,证明了传播的有效性,包括症状前和无症状传播,再加上临床上重大疾病,可以产生毁灭性的全球后果。甲型流感病毒由于其广泛的动物 储存宿主、抗原 进化 的能力以及人类大流行的历史,仍然是一个主要的大流行问题。尽管尚未确定持续的人传人 传播,但鉴于在已确诊的人类病例中观察到高病死率,高致病性甲型禽流感 (H5N1) 尤其令人担忧。相比之下,亨尼帕病毒和丝状病毒尽管传播能力相对有限,但由于其高致死率而构成重大临床威胁。本综述通过全球健康风险的三个相互关联的决定因素批判性地审查了主要的病毒威胁:传播性、感染死亡风险以及控制其流行和大流行潜力的生物和流行病学因素。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
因此,最大的全球威胁不一定是最具传染性或致命性的病毒,而是那些结合了有效的 传播、临床严重性、人畜共患 储存宿主、进化适应性和有限的预防或治疗选择的病毒。
证据基础与方法
提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。
共识与结论
甲型流感病毒由于其广泛的动物 储存宿主、抗原 进化 的能力以及人类大流行的历史,仍然是一个主要的大流行问题。
争议与缺口
相比之下,亨尼帕病毒和丝状病毒尽管传播能力相对有限,但由于其高致死率而构成重大临床威胁。
科研与实践启示
本综述通过全球健康风险的三个相互关联的决定因素批判性地审查了主要的病毒威胁:传播性、感染死亡风险以及控制其流行和大流行潜力的生物和流行病学因素。

Viral Pathogens as Global Health Threats: Review of Transmissibility, Lethality, and Pandemic Potential

Authors: Jonathan A. Coffman, Franklin Garcia-Godoy, Peter E. Murray, F. García-Godoy
Original abstract (English)
Viral pathogens vary substantially in their transmissibility, lethality, and potential to cause widespread epidemics or pandemics. Measles, for example, is exceptionally contagious but has relatively low case fatality in populations with access to modern medical care. Conversely, rabies, Nipah virus, Ebola virus, and Marburg virus can cause severe disease with high case fatality rates but generally exhibit limited sustained human-to-human transmission. The greatest global threats, therefore, are not necessarily the most contagious or lethal viruses, but those that combine efficient transmission, substantial clinical severity, zoonotic reservoirs, evolutionary adaptability, and limited preventive or therapeutic options. The COVID-19 pandemic illustrated this convergence of risk factors, demonstrating how efficient transmission, including presymptomatic and asymptomatic spread, coupled with clinically significant disease can produce devastating global consequences. Influenza A viruses remain a major pandemic concern because of their extensive animal reservoirs, capacity for antigenic evolution, and established history of human pandemics. Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) is particularly concerning given the high case fatality observed among confirmed human cases, although sustained human-to-human transmission has not been established. In contrast, henipaviruses and filoviruses pose substantial clinical threats because of their high lethality, despite comparatively limited transmissibility. This review critically examines major viral threats through three interconnected determinants of global health risk: transmissibility, infection fatality risk, and the biological and epidemiological factors governing their epidemic and pandemic potential.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The greatest global threats, therefore, are not necessarily the most contagious or lethal viruses, but those that combine efficient transmission, substantial clinical severity, zoonotic reservoirs, evolutionary adaptability, and limited preventive or therapeutic options.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
Influenza A viruses remain a major pandemic concern because of their extensive animal reservoirs, capacity for antigenic evolution, and established history of human pandemics.
Controversies and gaps
In contrast, henipaviruses and filoviruses pose substantial clinical threats because of their high lethality, despite comparatively limited transmissibility.
Research and practice implications
This review critically examines major viral threats through three interconnected determinants of global health risk: transmissibility, infection fatality risk, and the biological and epidemiological factors governing their epidemic and pandemic potential.

罗马尼亚罗姆人健康状况概览:范围界定审查

Overview of Health Status among Roma Population in Romania: A Scoping Review
作者： David Valentin Mangaloiu, Eugenia Claudia Bratu, Carmen Madalina Raris-Fierbinteanu, Florentina Furtunescu
摘要中文翻译背景:罗姆人被广泛认为是欧洲最大的少数民族,独立估计表明,罗马尼亚有多达 200 万人居住。尽管有充分记录的社会经济劣势,但之前的审查尚未综合有关罗马尼亚罗姆人健康状况的证据。目标:本次范围审查绘制了有关罗马尼亚罗姆人社区医疗保健获取、生活质量、慢性疾病负担和传染病暴露的文献。方法:使用 Joanna Briggs Institute 方法和 PRISMA-ScR 指南,我们对 PubMed/MEDLINE、Web of Science、Science Direct 进行系统检索,并辅以灰色文献和参考文献列表筛选,查找 2010-2025 年以英语或罗马尼亚语发表的研究。结果:39 个来源(35 项研究和 4 项机构报告)符合纳入标准。罗姆人社区的预期寿命差距为 3.5-4.6 年,预防性护理的接受率明显较低(普通人群为 39%,普通人群为 71%),而没有保险的比例很高(42-59%)。慢性病负担显着高于普通人群:高血压 患病率 为 33-67%,2 型糖尿病为 10-29%,肥胖为 32-62%,有证据表明发病更早,并发症更严重,包括更容易进展为慢性肾病和终末期肾病。罗姆人与健康相关的生活质量和心理健康指标均较差。关于传染病的证据很少,但表明乙型/丙型肝炎 麻疹 升高,麻疹 和 COVID-19 的严重程度更高。结论:在交叉的社会经济、结构和行为决定因素的推动下,罗马尼亚的罗姆人社区在几乎所有检查领域的健康状况均明显较差。
